`apps/api/build.mjs` (C-4 — bundle เพราะ workspace package ส่ง `.ts` ดิบ):

```js
// The workspace packages export TypeScript sources (packages/*/package.json → "exports": "./src/index.ts") and have
// no build step, so `tsc` alone produces a dist/ that Node cannot import. Bundling inlines @dayo/* and keeps the
// runtime dependencies external — which also keeps the cold start on Render free as small as possible.
import { build } from 'esbuild'

const shared = {
  bundle: true,
  platform: 'node',
  target: 'node22',
  format: 'esm',
  sourcemap: true,
  banner: { js: "import { createRequire as __cr } from 'node:module'; const require = __cr(import.meta.url);" },
  external: ['@nestjs/*', 'pg', 'reflect-metadata', 'rxjs', 'express', 'zod', 'drizzle-orm'],
  logLevel: 'info',
}

await build({ ...shared, entryPoints: ['src/main.ts'], outfile: 'dist/main.js' })
await build({ ...shared, entryPoints: ['src/migrate.ts'], outfile: 'dist/migrate.js' })
```

`apps/api/.env.example`:

```
# Neon (Singapore) POOLED connection string for normal queries — set in the Render dashboard, never in the repo
DATABASE_URL=postgresql://user:password@ep-example-pooler.ap-southeast-1.aws.neon.tech/dayo?sslmode=require
# DIRECT (non-pooled) connection string — same database, host WITHOUT "-pooler". Used only to hold the single-writer
# advisory lock on one long-lived session (T5-3). Through pgbouncer a session lock is meaningless, so this must not
# be the pooled host. If it is left empty the server still runs but refuses every write (and says so in the log).
DIRECT_DATABASE_URL=postgresql://user:password@ep-example.ap-southeast-1.aws.neon.tech/dayo?sslmode=require
# One-time code typed on the tablet's setup screen to register or revoke a device (Q5-9, Q5-18). 16+ characters.
REGISTRATION_CODE=change-me-to-a-long-random-string
# Comma-separated origins allowed to call the API (the Cloudflare Pages URL)
CORS_ORIGINS=https://dayo-pos.pages.dev
# Where the drizzle pg migration folder was copied to on the server (plan 5b deploy sets it)
MIGRATIONS_DIR=./drizzle/pg
PORT=3000
```

`apps/api/src/config.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod'

const Env = z.object({
  DATABASE_URL: z.string().min(1, 'DATABASE_URL is required'),
  DIRECT_DATABASE_URL: z.string().default(''),
  REGISTRATION_CODE: z.string().min(16, 'REGISTRATION_CODE must be at least 16 characters'),
  CORS_ORIGINS: z.string().default(''),
  PORT: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(65_535).default(3000),
  API_VERSION: z.string().default('plan-5'),
})

export type AppConfig = {
  databaseUrl: string
  /** Non-pooled connection used only for the single-writer advisory lock (T5-3); '' disables writing. */
  directDatabaseUrl: string
  registrationCode: string
  corsOrigins: string[]
  port: number
  apiVersion: string
}

export function loadConfig(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv): AppConfig {
  const parsed = Env.safeParse(env)
  if (!parsed.success) throw new Error(parsed.error.issues.map((i) => `${String(i.path[0] ?? '(root)')}: ${i.message}`).join(' · '))
  const e = parsed.data
  if (e.DIRECT_DATABASE_URL.includes('-pooler')) {
    // A pooled host cannot hold a session lock (T5-3) — fail loudly at boot instead of pretending to be safe.
    throw new Error('DIRECT_DATABASE_URL must be the non-pooled host (no "-pooler")')
  }
  return {
    databaseUrl: e.DATABASE_URL,
    directDatabaseUrl: e.DIRECT_DATABASE_URL,
    registrationCode: e.REGISTRATION_CODE,
    corsOrigins: e.CORS_ORIGINS.split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter((s) => s !== ''),
    port: e.PORT,
    apiVersion: e.API_VERSION,
  }
}

export const CONFIG = Symbol('CONFIG')
```

`apps/api/src/zod.pipe.ts`:

```ts
import { BadRequestException } from '@nestjs/common'
import type { ZodType } from 'zod'

/** Parses a body/query with zod and turns a failure into the shape the device understands. */
export function parseOrThrow<T>(schema: ZodType<T>, value: unknown): T {
  const parsed = schema.safeParse(value)
  if (!parsed.success) {
    const detail = parsed.error.issues.slice(0, 3).map((i) => `${i.path.join('.') || '(root)'}: ${i.message}`).join(' · ')
    throw new BadRequestException({ error: 'SCHEMA', detail })
  }
  return parsed.data
}
```

`apps/api/src/db/types.ts`:

```ts
import type { NodePgDatabase } from 'drizzle-orm/node-postgres'
import type { pg } from '@dayo/db-schema'

/** The one database type the whole API uses. Tests pass a PGlite drizzle instance cast to this type: both drivers
 *  implement the same query builder and the API never calls anything driver-specific. */
export type Db = NodePgDatabase<typeof pg>

export const DB = Symbol('DB')
export const WRITE_QUEUE = Symbol('WRITE_QUEUE')
export const SINGLE_WRITER = Symbol('SINGLE_WRITER')

/** Single-writer guarantee (T5-3): every write transaction runs one at a time inside this process, so a pull never
 *  sees a server_seq gap left by an in-flight transaction (spec §6.1). Same idea as the device's serial() queue. */
export type WriteQueue = <T>(fn: () => Promise<T>) => Promise<T>

export function createWriteQueue(): WriteQueue {
  let tail: Promise<unknown> = Promise.resolve()
  return <T>(fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> => {
    const run = tail.then(fn, fn)
    tail = run.then(() => undefined, () => undefined)
    return run
  }
}
```

`apps/api/src/db/single-writer.ts`:

```ts
import pgPkg from 'pg'

/**
 * Arbitrary but fixed: the advisory-lock id this deployment uses ("DAYO" in digits).
 * **Must stay below 2^32** — Postgres splits a bigint lock key into `classid` (high 32 bits) and `objid` (low 32),
 * and `ensureForWrite` queries `classid = 0 and objid = $1`. A larger value would make that check silently always
 * false, so every write would answer 503 for ever (F-m3).
 */
export const DAYO_WRITER_LOCK = 826_150_501

/**
 * T5-3: the pull cursor is only safe while one process writes. Render free runs a single instance, but a config slip
 * (or a second worker in a later plan) must not corrupt the cursor silently.
 *
 * `pg_try_advisory_lock` is a SESSION lock: it lives exactly as long as the connection that took it. Taking it
 * through the pool would hand the connection back and lose the lock the moment `idleTimeoutMillis` expires, while
 * this process still believed it held it — two writers, both certain they are alone (re-review N-2). So the lock
 * lives on its own `pg.Client`, opened once at boot and never returned to anyone, on a connection string that does
 * NOT go through Neon's pooler (pgbouncer in transaction mode gives a different backend per transaction, which
 * makes a session lock meaningless).
 *
 * There is deliberately NO heartbeat (re-review R-1). A `select 1` every minute would keep Neon's compute awake
 * around the clock — roughly 180 CU-hours a month against a 100-hour free quota — and would contradict D15, which
 * is the reason /health and /sync/inbox-flag were built to touch no database at all. Instead the lock is proven
 * right before a write: `ensureForWrite()` asks `pg_locks` whether this backend still holds it, and takes it again
 * if Neon suspended the compute in the meantime. One extra query per push batch, on a path that queries anyway,
 * and no window where the process believes it is the writer while the lock is gone.
 */
export class SingleWriterState {
  private client: InstanceType<typeof pgPkg.Client> | null = null
  private holder = false
  private forced: boolean | null = null
  private directUrl = ''
  private connecting: Promise<boolean> | null = null

  /** Called once from onApplicationBootstrap. Never throws: a server that cannot lock still serves /health. */
  async acquire(directUrl: string): Promise<boolean> {
    this.directUrl = directUrl                       // remembered so a later write can reconnect on its own (F-1)
    // F-m5: two pushes arriving together must not each open a client. The first one's promise is shared.
    if (this.connecting !== null) return this.connecting
    this.connecting = this.connect(directUrl).finally(() => { this.connecting = null })
    return this.connecting
  }

  private async connect(directUrl: string): Promise<boolean> {
    // F-m4: without a timeout a cold Neon could hang the push that triggered the reconnect.
    const client = new pgPkg.Client({ connectionString: directUrl, connectionTimeoutMillis: 10_000 })
    client.on('error', (e) => this.lost(`connection error: ${e.message}`))
    client.on('end', () => this.lost('connection closed'))
    try {
      await client.connect()
      this.client = client
      const res = await client.query<{ locked: boolean }>('select pg_try_advisory_lock($1) as locked', [DAYO_WRITER_LOCK])
      this.holder = res.rows[0]?.locked === true
      if (!this.holder) console.error('another writer holds the DA-YO writer lock — this instance will refuse writes')
      return this.holder
    } catch (e) {
      // F-i3: a failed connect at boot (Neon still cold) must not strand the process for ever — `lost` clears the
      // client so the next write reconnects instead of asking a session that was never established.
      this.lost(`could not take the lock: ${(e as Error).message}`)
      await client.end().catch(() => undefined)
      return false
    }
  }

  /**
   * Called before every write. Proves the lock is really held right now, reconnects when Neon suspended the compute
   * while the shop was closed, and takes the lock again on the new session — so a night of sleep never needs a
   * redeploy (F-1 · R-i4). `false` means another instance genuinely holds it, or the database cannot be reached:
   * the caller answers 503 and the device keeps its rows pending.
   */
  async ensureForWrite(): Promise<boolean> {
    if (this.forced !== null) return this.forced        // tests only: no real session to ask (see forceForTests)
    if (this.client === null) return this.reconnect()
    try {
      // objid holds the low 32 bits of the lock key; DAYO_WRITER_LOCK is below 2^32 so classid is 0 (F-m3).
      const held = await this.client.query<{ ok: boolean }>(
        `select exists(
           select 1 from pg_locks
           where locktype = 'advisory' and classid = 0 and objid = $1 and pid = pg_backend_pid()
         ) as ok`,
        [DAYO_WRITER_LOCK],
      )
      if (held.rows[0]?.ok === true) { this.holder = true; return true }
      const again = await this.client.query<{ locked: boolean }>('select pg_try_advisory_lock($1) as locked', [DAYO_WRITER_LOCK])
      this.holder = again.rows[0]?.locked === true
      if (!this.holder) console.error('another writer holds the DA-YO writer lock — refusing this write')
      return this.holder
    } catch (e) {
      this.lost(`lock check failed: ${(e as Error).message}`)
      return this.reconnect()                           // the session died under us: open a new one and try once
    }
  }

  private async reconnect(): Promise<boolean> {
    if (this.directUrl === '') return false             // never acquired: nothing to reconnect to
    return this.acquire(this.directUrl)
  }

  /** What /health reports (R-i5). Cheap and in-memory: it never queries. */
  isWriter(): boolean { return this.holder }

  private lost(why: string): void {
    if (this.holder) console.error(`DA-YO writer lock lost (${why}) — writes answer 503 until it is taken again`)
    this.holder = false
    this.client = null                                  // F-1: the next write reconnects instead of asking a corpse
  }

  async release(): Promise<void> {
    this.holder = false
    const client = this.client
    this.client = null
    await client?.end().catch(() => undefined)
  }

  /**
   * For tests only: the lock itself needs a real Postgres session (PGlite has nothing to ask), so tests pin the
   * answer here and exercise the 503 path instead. Once set, `ensureForWrite()` returns this value without any
   * query — which is also what keeps the test suite from needing a live database.
   */
  forceForTests(holder: boolean): void { this.forced = holder; this.holder = holder }
}
```

> **เทสต์ของกลไกนี้ทดสอบ "ทางเดินของ 503" และ "การต่อใหม่" ไม่ใช่ตัว lock บน Postgres จริง** — PGlite ไม่มี advisory lock ที่มีความหมายในบริบทนี้ · **สิ่งที่ต้องพิสูจน์ตอนแผน 5b deploy มี 4 ข้อ:** (1) ร้านปิดแล้วไม่มีคำสั่งใดยิง Neon เลย (ดู Neon compute ว่าหลับจริง) (2) compute restart แล้ว push ได้เองโดยไม่ต้อง redeploy (3) เปิดอินสแตนซ์ที่สองชั่วคราวแล้วต้องมีตัวเดียวที่เขียนได้ (4) **ปล่อยให้ Neon หลับข้ามคืน (ไม่ sync เลยตั้งแต่ปิดร้าน) แล้วเช้าวันรุ่งขึ้น push ครั้งแรกต้องผ่านเองโดยไม่ต้อง redeploy** — ข้อนี้คือข้อที่จะจับได้ว่าการต่อ session ใหม่ทำงานจริงหรือไม่ (F-1)

เทสต์ของการต่อใหม่ (ใส่ใน `apps/api/test/health.test.ts` — ไม่ต้องมี Postgres จริง เพราะทดสอบ **ลำดับการตัดสินใจ** ไม่ใช่ SQL):

```ts
describe('SingleWriterState reconnects after the database went to sleep (F-1)', () => {
  it('opens a new session and takes the lock again when the old client is gone', async () => {
    const { SingleWriterState } = await import('../src/db/single-writer.js')
    const writer = new SingleWriterState()
    let connects = 0
    // Stand-in for pg.Client: the first session answers once and then behaves like a closed connection,
    // exactly what Neon's scale-to-zero leaves behind overnight.
    const clients: { alive: boolean }[] = []
    ;(writer as unknown as { connect: (url: string) => Promise<boolean> }).connect = async () => {
      connects++
      const self = { alive: true }
      clients.push(self)
      ;(writer as unknown as { client: unknown; holder: boolean }).client = {
        query: async () => { if (!self.alive) throw new Error('Connection terminated'); return { rows: [{ ok: true, locked: true }] } },
        on: () => undefined,
        end: async () => undefined,
      }
      ;(writer as unknown as { holder: boolean }).holder = true
      return true
    }

    expect(await writer.acquire('postgres://direct')).toBe(true)
    expect(await writer.ensureForWrite()).toBe(true)
    expect(connects).toBe(1)

    clients[0]!.alive = false                      // Neon suspended the compute overnight
    expect(await writer.ensureForWrite()).toBe(true)   // the next morning's first push must just work
    expect(connects).toBe(2)
    expect(writer.isWriter()).toBe(true)
  })

  // G-i1: เคสข้างบนเข้าทาง catch เสมอ ส่วนเคสนี้พิสูจน์ "กิ่ง client === null" ซึ่งเป็นเส้นทางจริงของ
  // Neon ที่หลับข้ามคืน (event 'error'/'end' ยิง → lost() → client = null) ต้องมีทั้งสองเคส
  it('reconnects through the null branch after lost() cleared the dead client', async () => {
    const { SingleWriterState } = await import('../src/db/single-writer.js')
    const writer = new SingleWriterState()
    let connects = 0
    ;(writer as unknown as { connect: (url: string) => Promise<boolean> }).connect = async () => {
      connects++
      ;(writer as unknown as { client: unknown; holder: boolean }).client = {
        query: async () => ({ rows: [{ ok: true, locked: true }] }),
        on: () => undefined,
        end: async () => undefined,
      }
      ;(writer as unknown as { holder: boolean }).holder = true
      return true
    }

    expect(await writer.acquire('postgres://direct')).toBe(true)
    ;(writer as unknown as { lost: () => void }).lost()                 // what the 'end' event does overnight
    expect((writer as unknown as { client: unknown }).client).toBeNull() // pins `this.client = null` in lost()
    expect(writer.isWriter()).toBe(false)
    expect(await writer.ensureForWrite()).toBe(true)                     // pins the `client === null` branch
    expect(connects).toBe(2)
  })

  it('refuses instead of reconnecting when no direct url was ever configured', async () => {
    const { SingleWriterState } = await import('../src/db/single-writer.js')
    expect(await new SingleWriterState().ensureForWrite()).toBe(false)
  })
})
```

> เทสต์นี้แทนที่ `connect` ซึ่งเป็น private — เขียนแบบนี้เพราะสิ่งที่ต้องพิสูจน์คือ **`lost()` เคลียร์ client แล้ว `ensureForWrite` ต่อใหม่จริง** ไม่ใช่ว่า SQL ถูก · ถ้า TypeScript ไม่ยอมให้เข้าถึง ให้ประกาศ `connect` เป็น `protected` แล้วทำคลาสลูกในเทสต์ — **ห้ามลดเทสต์นี้ทิ้ง** มันคือสิ่งเดียวที่กัน F-1 ไม่ให้กลับมา
>
> **บังคับพิสูจน์ด้วย mutation ก่อนปิด Task 4 (G-i1):** เอา `this.client = null` ออกจาก `lost()` ครั้งหนึ่ง แล้วเอา `if (this.client === null) return this.reconnect()` ออกอีกครั้งหนึ่ง — **เทสต์ต้องตกทั้งสองครั้ง** ถ้าไม่ตก แปลว่าเทสต์ยังไม่ได้กัน F-1 จริง ให้เติม assertion จนตก · reviewer (opus) ของ Task 4 ต้องเห็นผลรันทั้งสองครั้งในรายงาน
>
> **G-m1:** ใน `connect()` ให้ `new pgPkg.Client(...)` และ `.on(...)` อยู่ **ข้างใน** `try` — ถ้า `DIRECT_DATABASE_URL` รูปแบบผิด constructor จะโยนออกมาจนบูตไม่ขึ้นเป็น crash loop ทั้งที่ docstring เขียนว่า "Never throws"

`apps/api/src/db/db.module.ts`:

```ts
import { Global, Module } from '@nestjs/common'
import { drizzle } from 'drizzle-orm/node-postgres'
import pgPkg from 'pg'
import { pg as schema } from '@dayo/db-schema'
import { CONFIG, loadConfig, type AppConfig } from '../config.js'
import { SingleWriterState } from './single-writer.js'
import { createWriteQueue, DB, SINGLE_WRITER, WRITE_QUEUE, type Db } from './types.js'

/**
 * `pg.Pool` connects lazily on the first query, so GET /health and GET /sync/inbox-flag never wake Neon
 * (D15 · spec §2 "ไม่แตะ DB (ให้ Neon หลับได้)"). di.test.ts pins this with a closed port.
 */
@Global()
@Module({
  providers: [
    { provide: CONFIG, useFactory: (): AppConfig => loadConfig(process.env) },
    {
      provide: DB,
      inject: [CONFIG],
      useFactory: (config: AppConfig): Db => {
        const pool = new pgPkg.Pool({ connectionString: config.databaseUrl, max: 3, idleTimeoutMillis: 30_000 })
        return drizzle(pool, { schema })
      },
    },
    { provide: WRITE_QUEUE, useFactory: createWriteQueue },
    { provide: SINGLE_WRITER, useFactory: (): SingleWriterState => new SingleWriterState() },
  ],
  exports: [CONFIG, DB, WRITE_QUEUE, SINGLE_WRITER],
})
export class DbModule {}
```

`apps/api/src/sync/inbox-flag.service.ts`:

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common'

/** spec §6.2: the tablet polls every 30 s and only pulls for real when this in-memory flag is set. Plan 6 (LINE)
 *  sets it when the server writes an order. It is deliberately not in the database — a poll must not wake Neon.
 *  `get` never clears (M-2): only an explicit ?clear=1 does, so reading the clock cannot swallow an order. */
@Injectable()
export class InboxFlagService {
  private flag = false
  set(v: boolean): void { this.flag = v }
  get(): boolean { return this.flag }
  take(): boolean { const v = this.flag; this.flag = false; return v }
}
```

`apps/api/src/health/health.controller.ts`:

```ts
import { Controller, Get, Inject, Query } from '@nestjs/common'
import type { HealthResponse, InboxFlagResponse } from '@dayo/contracts'
import { CONFIG, type AppConfig } from '../config.js'
import { SingleWriterState } from '../db/single-writer.js'
import { SINGLE_WRITER } from '../db/types.js'
import { InboxFlagService } from '../sync/inbox-flag.service.js'

@Controller()
export class HealthController {
  constructor(
    @Inject(CONFIG) private readonly config: AppConfig,
    @Inject(InboxFlagService) private readonly inbox: InboxFlagService,   // explicit: no decorator metadata (C-5)
    @Inject(SINGLE_WRITER) private readonly writer: SingleWriterState,
  ) {}

  /** Pinged every 10 minutes so Render free never sleeps (D15). Must stay database-free — `writer` is the
   *  in-memory flag, so this still answers without waking Neon while telling an operator why pushes fail (R-i5). */
  @Get('health')
  health(): HealthResponse {
    return { ok: true, serverTime: new Date().toISOString(), version: this.config.apiVersion, writer: this.writer.isWriter() }
  }

  @Get('sync/inbox-flag')
  inboxFlag(@Query('clear') clear?: string): InboxFlagResponse {
    const hasInbox = clear === '1' ? this.inbox.take() : this.inbox.get()
    return { hasInbox, serverTime: new Date().toISOString() }
  }
}
```

`apps/api/src/app.module.ts`:

```ts
import { Inject, Module, type OnApplicationBootstrap, type OnApplicationShutdown } from '@nestjs/common'
import { CONFIG, type AppConfig } from './config.js'
import { DbModule } from './db/db.module.js'
import { SingleWriterState } from './db/single-writer.js'
import { SINGLE_WRITER } from './db/types.js'
import { HealthController } from './health/health.controller.js'
import { InboxFlagService } from './sync/inbox-flag.service.js'

@Module({
  imports: [DbModule],
  controllers: [HealthController],
  providers: [InboxFlagService],
  exports: [InboxFlagService],
})
export class AppModule implements OnApplicationBootstrap, OnApplicationShutdown {
  constructor(
    @Inject(CONFIG) private readonly config: AppConfig,
    @Inject(SINGLE_WRITER) private readonly writer: SingleWriterState,
  ) {}

  /** T5-3: take the writer lock once, on its own connection. A failure never stops the server — /health must
   *  keep answering so the ping does not think the service is down — but every write will answer 503. */
  async onApplicationBootstrap(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.config.directDatabaseUrl === '') {
      console.error('DIRECT_DATABASE_URL is empty — this instance will refuse writes (T5-3)')
      return
    }
    await this.writer.acquire(this.config.directDatabaseUrl)
  }

  async onApplicationShutdown(): Promise<void> {
    await this.writer.release()
  }
}
```

> เทสต์ที่ override `SINGLE_WRITER` ด้วย `forceForTests(true)` จะไม่เข้าเส้นทาง `acquire` เลย (config ของเทสต์ตั้ง `directDatabaseUrl: ''`) จึงไม่ต้องมี Postgres จริงในเทสต์

`apps/api/src/main.ts`:

```ts
import 'reflect-metadata'
import { NestFactory } from '@nestjs/core'
import type { NestExpressApplication } from '@nestjs/platform-express'
import { AppModule } from './app.module.js'
import { loadConfig } from './config.js'

async function bootstrap(): Promise<void> {
  const config = loadConfig(process.env)
  const app = await NestFactory.create<NestExpressApplication>(AppModule, { logger: ['log', 'warn', 'error'] })
  app.useBodyParser('json', { limit: '2mb' })      // a 200-row push stays far below this
  if (config.corsOrigins.length > 0) app.enableCors({ origin: config.corsOrigins, methods: ['GET', 'POST'] })
  await app.listen(config.port, '0.0.0.0')
}

void bootstrap()
```

`apps/api/src/migrate.ts`:

```ts
import { drizzle } from 'drizzle-orm/node-postgres'
import { migrate } from 'drizzle-orm/node-postgres/migrator'
import pgPkg from 'pg'
import { PG_MIGRATIONS_FOLDER } from '@dayo/db-schema'
import { loadConfig } from './config.js'

/**
 * Runs before main.js (start command). PG_MIGRATIONS_FOLDER resolves against the db-schema *source* folder, which
 * exists in a repo checkout but not next to a bundled dist/ — MIGRATIONS_DIR overrides it when the deploy copies
 * the SQL files elsewhere (plan 5b). Always test on a Neon branch first (spec §10).
 */
async function main(): Promise<void> {
  const config = loadConfig(process.env)
  const folder = process.env['MIGRATIONS_DIR'] ?? PG_MIGRATIONS_FOLDER
  const pool = new pgPkg.Pool({ connectionString: config.databaseUrl, max: 1 })
  try {
    await migrate(drizzle(pool), { migrationsFolder: folder })
    console.log(`migrations applied from ${folder}`)
  } finally {
    await pool.end()
  }
}

main().catch((e: unknown) => { console.error(e); process.exit(1) })
```

`apps/api/test/helpers/api-harness.ts`:

```ts
import 'reflect-metadata'
import { PGlite } from '@electric-sql/pglite'
import { Test } from '@nestjs/testing'
import { drizzle } from 'drizzle-orm/pglite'
import request from 'supertest'
import type { INestApplication } from '@nestjs/common'
import type TestAgent from 'supertest/lib/agent.js'
import { migratePg, pg as schema } from '@dayo/db-schema'
import { AppModule } from '../../src/app.module.js'
import { CONFIG, type AppConfig } from '../../src/config.js'
import { SingleWriterState } from '../../src/db/single-writer.js'
import { createWriteQueue, DB, SINGLE_WRITER, WRITE_QUEUE, type Db } from '../../src/db/types.js'
import { InboxFlagService } from '../../src/sync/inbox-flag.service.js'

export const TEST_CONFIG: AppConfig = {
  databaseUrl: 'postgres://unused-in-tests',
  directDatabaseUrl: '',                 // empty on purpose: tests never take a real advisory lock (T5-3)
  registrationCode: 'test-registration-code-1234',
  corsOrigins: [],
  port: 0,
  apiVersion: 'test',
}

export type TestApi = {
  app: INestApplication
  http: TestAgent
  db: Db
  inboxFlag: InboxFlagService
  /** The overridden SingleWriterState — tests flip it to exercise the 503 path, not the lock itself. */
  writer: SingleWriterState
  /** Proves /health never queries (D15): counts every statement PGlite executes. */
  countQueries: { total: () => number; reset: () => void }
  close: () => Promise<void>
}

export async function createTestApi(): Promise<TestApi> {
  const client = new PGlite()
  let queries = 0
  for (const name of ['query', 'exec'] as const) {
    const original = client[name].bind(client) as (...a: unknown[]) => unknown
    ;(client as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)[name] = (...args: unknown[]) => { queries++; return original(...args) }
  }
  const db = drizzle(client, { schema }) as unknown as Db
  await migratePg(db as never)

  // PGlite has no advisory locks worth testing here; the state is forced so writes are allowed.
  const writer = new SingleWriterState()
  writer.forceForTests(true)

  const moduleRef = await Test.createTestingModule({ imports: [AppModule] })
    .overrideProvider(CONFIG).useValue(TEST_CONFIG)
    .overrideProvider(DB).useValue(db)
    .overrideProvider(WRITE_QUEUE).useValue(createWriteQueue())
    .overrideProvider(SINGLE_WRITER).useValue(writer)
    .compile()

  const app = moduleRef.createNestApplication()
  await app.init()
  return {
    app,
    http: request(app.getHttpServer()),
    db,
    inboxFlag: app.get(InboxFlagService),
    writer,
    countQueries: { total: () => queries, reset: () => { queries = 0 } },
    close: async () => { await app.close(); await client.close() },
  }
}
```

### Task 5: `apps/api` — ลงทะเบียนเครื่อง · token · เพิกถอนเครื่องที่หาย · guard 💰 review: opus

**Files:**

- Modify: `apps/api/src/app.module.ts`, `apps/api/test/helpers/api-harness.ts`

- Produces:
  - `newDeviceToken(): string` · `hashToken(t): string` · `MAX_ACTIVE_TOKENS_PER_DEVICE = 3` (M-10 · Q5-21)
  - `AuthService.register(req)` · `AuthService.authenticate(deviceId, token)` · `AuthService.revoke(req)` · `requireWriter()` (เส้นทางเขียนของ auth ก็ต้องถือ writer lock — F-i2) · `type DeviceIdentity = { deviceId: string; deviceName: string }`
  - `DeviceGuard` (`Authorization: Bearer` + `x-device-id`) · `@CurrentDevice()`

`apps/api/test/auth.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { eq } from 'drizzle-orm'
import { pg } from '@dayo/db-schema'
import { RegisterDeviceResponse } from '@dayo/contracts'
import { createTestApi, TEST_CONFIG, type TestApi } from './helpers/api-harness.js'

let api: TestApi
beforeAll(async () => { api = await createTestApi() })
afterAll(async () => { await api.close() })

const BODY = {
  registrationCode: TEST_CONFIG.registrationCode,
  deviceId: 'dev-1',
  deviceName: 'แท็บเล็ตหน้าร้าน',
  receiptPrefix: 'A',
  users: [{ id: 'u-1', displayName: 'TungAo', role: 'owner' as const, pinHash: '$argon2id$v=19$fake', isActive: true, createdAt: '2026-09-22T03:00:00.000Z' }],
  settings: [{ key: 'promptpay.id', valueJson: '0812345678', effectiveFrom: '2026-09-22T03:00:00.000Z' }],
}

describe('POST /auth/device/register', () => {
  it('refuses a wrong registration code', async () => {
    const res = await api.http.post('/auth/device/register').send({ ...BODY, registrationCode: 'wrong-code-123456' }).expect(401)
    expect(res.body.error).toBe('BAD_CODE')
  })

  it('refuses a body that does not match the schema', async () => {
    const res = await api.http.post('/auth/device/register').send({ ...BODY, receiptPrefix: 'aa' }).expect(400)
    expect(res.body.error).toBe('SCHEMA')
  })

  it('creates the device, its users and its settings, and returns a token', async () => {
    const res = await api.http.post('/auth/device/register').send(BODY).expect(201)
    const body = RegisterDeviceResponse.parse(res.body)
    expect(body.deviceId).toBe('dev-1')
    expect(body.token.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(32)
    expect((await api.db.select().from(pg.device).where(eq(pg.device.id, 'dev-1')))[0]?.receiptPrefix).toBe('A')
    expect(await api.db.select().from(pg.user)).toHaveLength(1)
    expect((await api.db.select().from(pg.setting))[0]?.key).toBe('promptpay.id')
  })

  it('stores only the hash of the token, never the token itself', async () => {
    const rows = await api.db.select().from(pg.deviceToken).where(eq(pg.deviceToken.deviceId, 'dev-1'))
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(rows[0]?.tokenHash).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/)
  })

  it('writes an audit_log row for the registration (D21)', async () => {
    const rows = await api.db.select().from(pg.auditLog).where(eq(pg.auditLog.entityId, 'dev-1'))
    expect(rows.map((r) => r.action)).toContain('register')
  })

  it('is idempotent about the rows: registering again adds a token but no duplicate device or user', async () => {
    await api.http.post('/auth/device/register').send(BODY).expect(201)
    expect(await api.db.select().from(pg.device)).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(await api.db.select().from(pg.user)).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it('keeps at most three live tokens per device and revokes the oldest (M-10 · Q5-21)', async () => {
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) await api.http.post('/auth/device/register').send(BODY).expect(201)
    const rows = await api.db.select().from(pg.deviceToken).where(eq(pg.deviceToken.deviceId, 'dev-1'))
    expect(rows.filter((r) => r.revokedAt === null).length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(3)
    expect(rows.length).toBeGreaterThan(3)                       // history is kept, nothing is deleted
  })

  it('refuses a second device that wants a receipt prefix already in use', async () => {
    const res = await api.http.post('/auth/device/register').send({ ...BODY, deviceId: 'dev-2', users: [], settings: [] }).expect(409)
    expect(res.body.error).toBe('PREFIX_TAKEN')
  })
})

describe('POST /auth/device', () => {
  it('accepts a real token', async () => {
    const { body } = await api.http.post('/auth/device/register').send({ ...BODY, deviceId: 'dev-3', receiptPrefix: 'C', users: [], settings: [] }).expect(201)
    const res = await api.http.post('/auth/device').send({ deviceId: 'dev-3', token: body.token }).expect(200)
    expect(res.body.deviceName).toBe('แท็บเล็ตหน้าร้าน')
  })

  it('refuses a token that belongs to another device', async () => {
    const { body } = await api.http.post('/auth/device/register').send({ ...BODY, deviceId: 'dev-4', receiptPrefix: 'D', users: [], settings: [] }).expect(201)
    await api.http.post('/auth/device').send({ deviceId: 'dev-1', token: body.token }).expect(401)
  })

  it('refuses a made-up token', async () => {
    await api.http.post('/auth/device').send({ deviceId: 'dev-1', token: 'x'.repeat(43) }).expect(401)
  })

  it('records when the device last talked to the server (N-m7)', async () => {
    const { body } = await api.http.post('/auth/device/register').send({ ...BODY, deviceId: 'dev-7', receiptPrefix: 'G', users: [], settings: [] }).expect(201)
    expect((await api.db.select().from(pg.deviceToken).where(eq(pg.deviceToken.deviceId, 'dev-7')))[0]?.lastUsedAt).toBeNull()
    await api.http.post('/auth/device').send({ deviceId: 'dev-7', token: body.token }).expect(200)
    expect((await api.db.select().from(pg.deviceToken).where(eq(pg.deviceToken.deviceId, 'dev-7')))[0]?.lastUsedAt).toMatch(/^\d{4}-/)
  })
})

describe('POST /auth/device/revoke (Q5-18: the tablet was lost)', () => {
  it('needs the registration code', async () => {
    await api.http.post('/auth/device/revoke').send({ registrationCode: 'wrong-code-123456', deviceId: 'dev-3' }).expect(401)
  })

  it('cuts off every live token of that device and says how many', async () => {
    const { body } = await api.http.post('/auth/device/register').send({ ...BODY, deviceId: 'dev-5', receiptPrefix: 'E', users: [], settings: [] }).expect(201)
    const res = await api.http.post('/auth/device/revoke').send({ registrationCode: TEST_CONFIG.registrationCode, deviceId: 'dev-5' }).expect(200)
    expect(res.body.revoked).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1)
    await api.http.post('/auth/device').send({ deviceId: 'dev-5', token: body.token }).expect(401)
  })

  it('writes an audit_log row and leaves the other devices alone', async () => {
    const rows = await api.db.select().from(pg.auditLog).where(eq(pg.auditLog.entityId, 'dev-5'))
    expect(rows.map((r) => r.action)).toContain('revoke')
    const { body } = await api.http.post('/auth/device/register').send({ ...BODY, deviceId: 'dev-6', receiptPrefix: 'F', users: [], settings: [] }).expect(201)
    await api.http.post('/auth/device').send({ deviceId: 'dev-6', token: body.token }).expect(200)
  })

  it('answers 0 for a device that has no live token, without failing', async () => {
    const res = await api.http.post('/auth/device/revoke').send({ registrationCode: TEST_CONFIG.registrationCode, deviceId: 'dev-unknown' }).expect(200)
    expect(res.body.revoked).toBe(0)
  })
})

describe('registration and revocation need the writer lock too (F-i2)', () => {
  it('refuses to register when this instance is not the writer', async () => {
    api.writer.forceForTests(false)
    await api.http.post('/auth/device/register').send({ ...BODY, deviceId: 'dev-nw', receiptPrefix: 'Z', users: [], settings: [] }).expect(503)
    expect(await api.db.select().from(pg.device).where(eq(pg.device.id, 'dev-nw'))).toHaveLength(0)
    api.writer.forceForTests(true)
  })

  it('refuses to revoke when this instance is not the writer', async () => {
    api.writer.forceForTests(false)
    await api.http.post('/auth/device/revoke').send({ registrationCode: TEST_CONFIG.registrationCode, deviceId: 'dev-1' }).expect(503)
    api.writer.forceForTests(true)
  })
})
```

`apps/api/src/auth/tokens.ts`:

```ts
import { randomBytes } from 'node:crypto'
import { sha256Hex } from '@dayo/domain'

/** 32 random bytes as base64url — 256 bits of entropy, no padding, safe inside a header. */
export function newDeviceToken(): string {
  return randomBytes(32).toString('base64url')
}

/** Only the hash is stored (Task 3): a database dump must not be replayable as a login. */
export function hashToken(token: string): string {
  return sha256Hex(token)
}

/** M-10 · Q5-21: a device keeps at most this many live tokens; registering again revokes the oldest. */
export const MAX_ACTIVE_TOKENS_PER_DEVICE = 3

/** N-m7: how stale device_token.last_used_at may get before the next authenticated call refreshes it. */
export const LAST_USED_WRITE_EVERY_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000
```

`apps/api/src/auth/auth.service.ts`:

```ts
import { ConflictException, Inject, Injectable, ServiceUnavailableException, UnauthorizedException } from '@nestjs/common'
import { and, asc, eq, isNull, ne } from 'drizzle-orm'
import { pg as s } from '@dayo/db-schema'
import type { RegisterDeviceRequest, RegisterDeviceResponse, RevokeDeviceRequest, RevokeDeviceResponse } from '@dayo/contracts'
import { CONFIG, type AppConfig } from '../config.js'
import { SingleWriterState } from '../db/single-writer.js'
import { DB, SINGLE_WRITER, WRITE_QUEUE, type Db, type WriteQueue } from '../db/types.js'
import { hashToken, LAST_USED_WRITE_EVERY_MS, MAX_ACTIVE_TOKENS_PER_DEVICE, newDeviceToken } from './tokens.js'

export type DeviceIdentity = { deviceId: string; deviceName: string }

@Injectable()
export class AuthService {
  constructor(
    @Inject(DB) private readonly db: Db,
    @Inject(WRITE_QUEUE) private readonly write: WriteQueue,
    @Inject(CONFIG) private readonly config: AppConfig,
    @Inject(SINGLE_WRITER) private readonly writer: SingleWriterState,
  ) {}

  /**
   * F-i2: `device`, `user` and `setting` all carry `server_seq`, so writing them from an instance that does not
   * hold the writer lock would open exactly the cursor gap T5-3 exists to prevent — and Global Constraints promise
   * "no lock = every write answers 503" without qualification. Registration is also the moment most likely to find
   * Neon asleep, which is why `ensureForWrite()` (not `isWriter()`) is used: it wakes the connection and takes the
   * lock again. If that first attempt still times out on a cold start, the tablet shows
   * "ต่อเซิร์ฟเวอร์ไม่ได้" and pressing the button again succeeds — see Task 12 Step 4.
   */
  private async requireWriter(): Promise<void> {
    if (!(await this.writer.ensureForWrite())) throw new ServiceUnavailableException({ error: 'NOT_WRITER' })
  }

  /**
   * Q5-9 · Q5-10 · spec §6.4: the tablet's setup screen already created the device, the owners (pin_hash only) and
   * the PromptPay setting locally; this call copies those rows up once and hands back a long-lived token. From then
   * on the server owns them (type R) and the device pulls them back down.
   */
  async register(req: RegisterDeviceRequest): Promise<RegisterDeviceResponse> {
    if (req.registrationCode !== this.config.registrationCode) throw new UnauthorizedException({ error: 'BAD_CODE' })
    await this.requireWriter()
    const token = newDeviceToken()
    const tokenHash = hashToken(token)
    const at = new Date().toISOString()

    await this.write(async () => {
      const clash = await this.db.select({ id: s.device.id }).from(s.device)
        .where(and(eq(s.device.receiptPrefix, req.receiptPrefix), ne(s.device.id, req.deviceId))).limit(1)
      if (clash.length > 0) throw new ConflictException({ error: 'PREFIX_TAKEN', detail: req.receiptPrefix })

      await this.db.transaction(async (tx) => {
        await tx.insert(s.device)
          .values({ id: req.deviceId, name: req.deviceName, receiptPrefix: req.receiptPrefix, isSellingDevice: true, registeredAt: at, updatedAt: at, version: 1 })
          .onConflictDoUpdate({ target: s.device.id, set: { name: req.deviceName, updatedAt: at } })
        for (const u of req.users) {
          await tx.insert(s.user)
            .values({ id: u.id, displayName: u.displayName, role: u.role, pinHash: u.pinHash, isActive: u.isActive, createdAt: u.createdAt, updatedAt: at, version: 1 })
            .onConflictDoNothing({ target: s.user.id })
        }
        for (const st of req.settings) {
          await tx.insert(s.setting)
            .values({ key: st.key, valueJson: st.valueJson, effectiveFrom: st.effectiveFrom, updatedAt: at, version: 1 })
            .onConflictDoNothing({ target: [s.setting.key, s.setting.effectiveFrom] })
        }

        // M-10: keep the live tokens bounded, oldest first, and never delete history.
        const live = await tx.select({ id: s.deviceToken.id }).from(s.deviceToken)
          .where(and(eq(s.deviceToken.deviceId, req.deviceId), isNull(s.deviceToken.revokedAt)))
          .orderBy(asc(s.deviceToken.createdAt), asc(s.deviceToken.id))
        for (const old of live.slice(0, Math.max(0, live.length + 1 - MAX_ACTIVE_TOKENS_PER_DEVICE))) {
          await tx.update(s.deviceToken).set({ revokedAt: at }).where(eq(s.deviceToken.id, old.id))
        }

        await tx.insert(s.deviceToken).values({ id: `tok-${tokenHash.slice(0, 24)}`, deviceId: req.deviceId, tokenHash, createdAt: at, lastUsedAt: null, revokedAt: null })
        await tx.insert(s.auditLog).values({
          id: `aud-reg-${tokenHash.slice(0, 20)}`, entity: 'device', entityId: req.deviceId, action: 'register',
          beforeJson: null, afterJson: { name: req.deviceName, receiptPrefix: req.receiptPrefix, users: req.users.length, settings: req.settings.length },
          actorUserId: req.users[0]?.id ?? null, at,
        })
      })
    })

    return { deviceId: req.deviceId, token, serverTime: at }
  }

  /** Used by POST /auth/device and by DeviceGuard on every sync call. */
  async authenticate(deviceId: string, token: string): Promise<DeviceIdentity> {
    const tokenHash = hashToken(token)
    const rows = await this.db
      .select({ id: s.deviceToken.id, deviceId: s.deviceToken.deviceId, name: s.device.name, lastUsedAt: s.deviceToken.lastUsedAt })
      .from(s.deviceToken)
      .innerJoin(s.device, eq(s.device.id, s.deviceToken.deviceId))
      .where(and(eq(s.deviceToken.tokenHash, tokenHash), eq(s.deviceToken.deviceId, deviceId), isNull(s.deviceToken.revokedAt)))
      .limit(1)
    const row = rows[0]
    if (!row) throw new UnauthorizedException({ error: 'BAD_TOKEN' })
    // N-m7: "when did this tablet last talk to us" is exactly the question asked when one goes missing. Written at
    // most once every 5 minutes so a busy sync does not turn every request into a write.
    // R-m4 — a deliberate exception to "every write goes through WriteQueue": `device_token` has no `server_seq`,
    // so it cannot affect a pull cursor, and it holds no money. Routing it through the queue would put a write in
    // front of every authenticated request, including the ones that only read. Do not copy this exception.
    const now = Date.now()
    if (row.lastUsedAt === null || now - Date.parse(row.lastUsedAt) > LAST_USED_WRITE_EVERY_MS) {
      await this.db.update(s.deviceToken).set({ lastUsedAt: new Date(now).toISOString() }).where(eq(s.deviceToken.id, row.id))
    }
    return { deviceId: row.deviceId, deviceName: row.name }
  }

  /** Q5-18: the tablet was lost. Anyone with the registration code can cut it off from any other device. */
  async revoke(req: RevokeDeviceRequest): Promise<RevokeDeviceResponse> {
    if (req.registrationCode !== this.config.registrationCode) throw new UnauthorizedException({ error: 'BAD_CODE' })
    await this.requireWriter()
    const at = new Date().toISOString()
    const revoked = await this.write(async () =>
      this.db.transaction(async (tx) => {
        const live = await tx.select({ id: s.deviceToken.id }).from(s.deviceToken)
          .where(and(eq(s.deviceToken.deviceId, req.deviceId), isNull(s.deviceToken.revokedAt)))
        for (const row of live) await tx.update(s.deviceToken).set({ revokedAt: at }).where(eq(s.deviceToken.id, row.id))
        if (live.length > 0) {
          await tx.insert(s.auditLog).values({
            id: `aud-rev-${req.deviceId}-${at}`, entity: 'device', entityId: req.deviceId, action: 'revoke',
            beforeJson: { liveTokens: live.length }, afterJson: { liveTokens: 0 }, actorUserId: null, at,
          })
        }
        return live.length
      }),
    )
    return { deviceId: req.deviceId, revoked, serverTime: at }
  }
}
```

`apps/api/src/auth/device.guard.ts`:

```ts
import { CanActivate, createParamDecorator, ExecutionContext, Inject, Injectable, UnauthorizedException } from '@nestjs/common'
import type { Request } from 'express'
import { AuthService, type DeviceIdentity } from './auth.service.js'

type AuthedRequest = Request & { device?: DeviceIdentity }

@Injectable()
export class DeviceGuard implements CanActivate {
  constructor(@Inject(AuthService) private readonly auth: AuthService) {}

  async canActivate(ctx: ExecutionContext): Promise<boolean> {
    const req = ctx.switchToHttp().getRequest<AuthedRequest>()
    const header = req.headers.authorization ?? ''
    const token = header.startsWith('Bearer ') ? header.slice('Bearer '.length) : ''
    const deviceId = String(req.headers['x-device-id'] ?? '')
    if (token === '' || deviceId === '') throw new UnauthorizedException({ error: 'BAD_TOKEN' })
    req.device = await this.auth.authenticate(deviceId, token)
    return true
  }
}

export const CurrentDevice = createParamDecorator((_data: unknown, ctx: ExecutionContext): DeviceIdentity => {
  const req = ctx.switchToHttp().getRequest<AuthedRequest>()
  if (!req.device) throw new UnauthorizedException({ error: 'BAD_TOKEN' })
  return req.device
})
```

`apps/api/src/auth/auth.controller.ts`:

```ts
import { Body, Controller, HttpCode, Inject, Post } from '@nestjs/common'
import {
  AuthDeviceRequest, RegisterDeviceRequest, RevokeDeviceRequest,
  type AuthDeviceResponse, type RegisterDeviceResponse, type RevokeDeviceResponse,
} from '@dayo/contracts'
import { parseOrThrow } from '../zod.pipe.js'
import { AuthService } from './auth.service.js'

@Controller('auth')
export class AuthController {
  constructor(@Inject(AuthService) private readonly auth: AuthService) {}

  @Post('device/register')
  async register(@Body() body: unknown): Promise<RegisterDeviceResponse> {
    return this.auth.register(parseOrThrow(RegisterDeviceRequest, body))
  }

  @Post('device')
  @HttpCode(200)
  async authDevice(@Body() body: unknown): Promise<AuthDeviceResponse> {
    const req = parseOrThrow(AuthDeviceRequest, body)
    const identity = await this.auth.authenticate(req.deviceId, req.token)
    return { deviceId: identity.deviceId, deviceName: identity.deviceName, serverTime: new Date().toISOString() }
  }

  @Post('device/revoke')
  @HttpCode(200)
  async revoke(@Body() body: unknown): Promise<RevokeDeviceResponse> {
    return this.auth.revoke(parseOrThrow(RevokeDeviceRequest, body))
  }
}
```

```ts
@Module({ controllers: [AuthController], providers: [AuthService, DeviceGuard], exports: [AuthService, DeviceGuard] })
```

```ts
/** Registers a device and returns headers that pass DeviceGuard. */
export async function registerTestDevice(
  api: TestApi,
  over: Partial<{ deviceId: string; receiptPrefix: string; deviceName: string }> = {},
): Promise<{ deviceId: string; token: string; headers: Record<string, string> }> {

    registrationCode: TEST_CONFIG.registrationCode,
    deviceId,
    deviceName: over.deviceName ?? 'แท็บเล็ตทดสอบ',
    receiptPrefix: over.receiptPrefix ?? 'A',
    users: [],
    settings: [],
  }).expect(201)
  const token = String(res.body.token)
  return { deviceId, token, headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'x-device-id': deviceId } }
}
```

### Task 6: `apps/api` — `POST /sync/push` ชั้นรับเข้า (idempotency ต่อคีย์ · FK · แม่ยังไม่มา · เจ้าของแถว) 💰 review: opus

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/api/src/sync/tables.ts`, `apps/api/src/sync/push.service.ts`, `apps/api/src/sync/sync.controller.ts`, `apps/api/src/sync/sync.module.ts`, `apps/api/test/helpers/rows.ts`, `apps/api/test/push-ingest.test.ts`
- Modify: `apps/api/src/app.module.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `DB`, `WRITE_QUEUE`, `SINGLE_WRITER`, `DeviceGuard`, `@CurrentDevice()` · `PushRequest`, `PushResponse`, `RowResult`, `SYNC_ROW_SCHEMAS`, `ALLOWED_KEY_SUFFIX`, `parseIdempotencyKey`, `SyncTable` (contracts) · `canonicalJson`, `sha256Hex`, `statusTransitionAllowed` (domain) · `pg.*`
- Produces:
  - `PG_TABLE_OF: Record<SyncTable, PgTable>` · `PARENT_OF` · `DEVICE_FIELD_OF` · `orderIdOf(table, row)` · `STATUS_FIELD_OF`
  - `class RowRejected extends Error { verdict: Verdict }` — ใช้ throw เพื่อ rollback ทั้ง transaction (C-2)
  - `ROW_ERROR_VERDICT` (รหัสข้อผิดพลาดของ pg → verdict — N-1) · `pgErrorCode(e)`
  - `type Verdict = Omit<RowResult, 'idempotencyKey'>` · `PushService.ingest(deviceId, req): Promise<PushResponse>`
  - token `ROW_VERIFIER` + `type RowVerifier` (`verifyRow` · `rebuildCostStates`) — Task 6 ผูกกับตัวที่ **โยน** · Task 7 เปลี่ยนเป็น `VerifyService` (N-i2)
  - เทสต์: `helpers/rows.ts` → `seedReferenceRows(db)` · `orderDoc(over?)` · `AT` · `createTestApi({ verifier: 'passthrough' })`

> **C-2:** การเขียนแถว + บันทึก `idempotency_record` + การตรวจทุกอย่าง อยู่ใน `db.transaction` เดียวกัน · verdict ที่ไม่ผ่าน `throw new RowRejected(verdict)` เพื่อให้ rollback แล้วจับมาเป็นคำตอบ — **ห้ามมีเส้นทางใดที่ตอบ `rejected` แล้วแถวยังอยู่ในฐานข้อมูล**
> **C-3:** การกันส่งซ้ำอยู่ที่ **คีย์เต็ม** ใน `idempotency_record` · คีย์ที่ไม่มี suffix = insert-if-absent เสมอ · คีย์ที่มี suffix ที่อนุญาต = update ได้ครั้งเดียวและต้องเป็นทิศทางสถานะที่ถูก · suffix อื่น = `BAD_KEY`

`apps/api/test/helpers/rows.ts`:

```ts
import { computeEventHash, GENESIS_HASH } from '@dayo/domain'
import { pg } from '@dayo/db-schema'
import type { PushRow } from '@dayo/contracts'
import type { Db } from '../../src/db/types.js'

export const AT = '2026-09-22T03:00:00.000Z'

/** The minimum reference rows a pushed bill points at. A real server gets these from the Excel seed
 *  (canonicalSeedOpts — plan 3 §5); the tests only need the FK targets to exist. */
export async function seedReferenceRows(db: Db): Promise<void> {
  await db.insert(pg.user).values({ id: 'u-1', displayName: 'TungAo', role: 'owner', pinHash: 'x'.repeat(20), isActive: true, createdAt: AT, updatedAt: AT, version: 1 }).onConflictDoNothing()
  await db.insert(pg.channel).values({ id: 'ch-1', code: 'WALK_IN', name: 'หน้าร้าน', isActive: true, updatedAt: AT, version: 1 }).onConflictDoNothing()
  await db.insert(pg.category).values({ id: 'cat-1', code: 'TEA', name: 'ชาไทย', sort: 1, isActive: true, updatedAt: AT, version: 1 }).onConflictDoNothing()
  await db.insert(pg.item).values({ id: 'it-1', code: 'RM-TEA-01', name: 'ชาไทยผง', kind: 'raw', category: 'ของดิบ', useUnit: 'g', isTracked: true, reorderPointMilli: 500_000, standardCostUsat: 19_000_000, shelfLifeHours: null, isActive: true, note: null, updatedAt: AT, version: 1 }).onConflictDoNothing()
  await db.insert(pg.product).values({ id: 'pr-1', code: 'P-TEA-01', categoryId: 'cat-1', name: 'ชาไทย', prepGroup: null, soldOutUntil: null, isActive: true, sort: 1, updatedAt: AT, version: 1 }).onConflictDoNothing()
  await db.insert(pg.size).values({ id: 'sz-1', code: '16OZ', name: '16 oz', ml: 473, sort: 1, isActive: true, updatedAt: AT, version: 1 }).onConflictDoNothing()
  await db.insert(pg.productVariant).values({ id: 'pv-1', productId: 'pr-1', sizeId: 'sz-1', sku: 'Original-16oz', isActive: true, updatedAt: AT, version: 1 }).onConflictDoNothing()
  await db.insert(pg.sweetnessLevel).values({ id: 'sw-1', code: 'S050', name: 'หวาน 50%', percent: 50, sort: 3, isActive: true, updatedAt: AT, version: 1 }).onConflictDoNothing()
}

/**
 * One walk-in bill exactly as plans 3–4 queue it: shift → order → line → payment → event → movement.
 * The event hash is computed over `payload` (the EventCore field), while the row carries `payloadJson` —
 * the same split the device makes in apps/pos/src/db/events.ts (C-1).
 */
/**
 * A distinct small number per order id, so receipt_no and queue_no never clash between bills in a test — both are
 * unique per device in the schema (N-i1). A map, not a hash: numbers are handed out in first-asked order, so adding
 * an id later can never silently collide with an existing one (R-m1). Stable within a test file, which is what the
 * idempotency tests need: calling orderDoc twice for the same id returns byte-identical rows.
 */
const queueNumbers = new Map<string, number>()
function queueNumberFor(orderId: string): number {
  const known = queueNumbers.get(orderId)
  if (known !== undefined) return known
  const next = queueNumbers.size + 1
  queueNumbers.set(orderId, next)
  return next
}

export function orderDoc(over: Partial<{ orderId: string; totalSatang: number; deviceId: string; shiftId: string; suffix: string }> = {}): PushRow[] {
  const orderId = over.orderId ?? 'o-1'
  const deviceId = over.deviceId ?? 'dev-1'
  const shiftId = over.shiftId ?? 'sh-1'
  const total = over.totalSatang ?? 6500
  const s = over.suffix ?? ''                                   // lets a caller build a second, independent bill
  // N-i1: receipt_no AND queue_no are both unique per device (per business date for the queue — D47 item 5), so two
  // bills in one test may never share them.
  const n = queueNumberFor(orderId)
  const core = { orderId, seq: 1, deviceId, chainId: deviceId, chainSeq: 1, type: 'PAID' as const, payload: { totalSatang: total }, actorType: 'user' as const, actorId: 'u-1', at: AT }
  const hash = computeEventHash(GENESIS_HASH, core)
  return [
    { idempotencyKey: `shift:${shiftId}`, table: 'shift', row: { id: shiftId, deviceId, businessDate: '2026-09-22', status: 'open', openedBy: 'u-1', openedAt: AT, openingFloatSatang: 100_000, closedBy: null, closedAt: null } },
    { idempotencyKey: `order:${orderId}`, table: 'order', row: { id: orderId, origin: 'device', deviceId, receiptNo: `A-${String(n).padStart(6, '0')}`, queueNo: n, businessDate: '2026-09-22', shiftId, channelId: 'ch-1', customerId: null, status: 'paid', subtotalSatang: total, discountSatang: 0, totalSatang: total, vatSatang: 0, costSatang: 1800, note: null, createdByType: 'user', createdById: 'u-1', createdAt: AT, paidAt: AT, readyAt: null, voidedAt: null } },
    { idempotencyKey: `order_line:ol${s}-1`, table: 'order_line', row: { id: `ol${s}-1`, orderId, lineNo: 1, variantId: 'pv-1', sweetnessId: 'sw-1', recipeId: null, productName: 'ชาไทย', sizeName: '16 oz', sweetnessName: 'หวาน 50%', unitPriceSatang: total, qty: 1, lineTotalSatang: total, unitCostSatang: 1800 } },
    { idempotencyKey: `payment:pay${s}-1`, table: 'payment', row: { id: `pay${s}-1`, orderId, method: 'CASH', amountSatang: total, tenderedSatang: 10_000, changeSatang: 10_000 - total, reference: null, verifyStatus: 'manual', createdBy: 'u-1', createdAt: AT } },
    { idempotencyKey: `order_event:ev${s}-1`, table: 'order_event', row: { id: `ev${s}-1`, orderId, seq: 1, deviceId, chainId: deviceId, chainSeq: 1, type: 'PAID', payloadJson: { totalSatang: total }, actorType: 'user', actorId: 'u-1', at: AT, prevHash: GENESIS_HASH, hash } },
    { idempotencyKey: `stock_movement:mv${s}-1`, table: 'stock_movement', row: { id: `mv${s}-1`, itemId: 'it-1', kind: 'SALE', qtyMilli: -12_000, unitCostUsat: 19_000_000, refType: 'order', refId: orderId, businessDate: '2026-09-22', deviceId, createdBy: 'u-1', createdAt: AT } },
  ]
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: เขียนเทสต์ให้ตก**

`apps/api/test/push-ingest.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { eq } from 'drizzle-orm'
import { pg } from '@dayo/db-schema'
import { PushResponse } from '@dayo/contracts'
import { createTestApi, registerTestDevice, type TestApi } from './helpers/api-harness.js'
import { AT, orderDoc, seedReferenceRows } from './helpers/rows.js'

let api: TestApi
let auth: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof registerTestDevice>>
beforeAll(async () => {
  // This file tests the ingest layer on its own, so the row verifier of Task 7 is replaced by a pass-through.
  // The production default refuses (N-i2) — `push-verify.test.ts` runs against the real one.
  api = await createTestApi({ verifier: 'passthrough' })
  auth = await registerTestDevice(api)
  await seedReferenceRows(api.db)
})
afterAll(async () => { await api.close() })

const push = (rows: unknown[]) => api.http.post('/sync/push').set(auth.headers).send({ deviceId: auth.deviceId, rows })

describe('POST /sync/push', () => {
  it('refuses a call without a device token', async () => {
    await api.http.post('/sync/push').send({ deviceId: 'dev-1', rows: orderDoc() }).expect(401)
  })

  it('accepts a whole bill and writes every row', async () => {
    const body = PushResponse.parse((await push(orderDoc()).expect(201)).body)
    expect(body.results.map((r) => r.status)).toEqual(['accepted', 'accepted', 'accepted', 'accepted', 'accepted', 'accepted'])
    expect(await api.db.select().from(pg.order).where(eq(pg.order.id, 'o-1'))).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(await api.db.select().from(pg.orderEvent)).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it('fills server_received_at on every transaction row it writes', async () => {
    const rows = await api.db.select().from(pg.order).where(eq(pg.order.id, 'o-1'))
    expect(rows[0]?.serverReceivedAt).toMatch(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}T/)
  })

  it('is idempotent: sending the same batch three times changes nothing (spec §8)', async () => {
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      const body = PushResponse.parse((await push(orderDoc()).expect(201)).body)
      expect(body.results.every((r) => r.status === 'duplicate')).toBe(true)
    }
    expect(await api.db.select().from(pg.order)).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(await api.db.select().from(pg.payment)).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(await api.db.select().from(pg.stockMovement)).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it('refuses the same key with different content instead of overwriting it (C-3)', async () => {
    const [, order] = orderDoc()
    const tampered = { ...order!, row: { ...(order!.row as object), totalSatang: 9900 } }
    const res = await push([tampered]).expect(201)
    expect(res.body.results[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'rejected', reason: 'CONFLICT' })
    expect((await api.db.select().from(pg.order).where(eq(pg.order.id, 'o-1')))[0]?.totalSatang).toBe(6500)
  })

  it('takes shift:<id>:closed once and refuses a different second closing', async () => {
    const closed = { idempotencyKey: 'shift:sh-1:closed', table: 'shift', row: { id: 'sh-1', deviceId: auth.deviceId, businessDate: '2026-09-22', status: 'closed', openedBy: 'u-1', openedAt: AT, openingFloatSatang: 100_000, closedBy: 'u-1', closedAt: '2026-09-22T12:00:00.000Z' } }
    expect((await push([closed]).expect(201)).body.results[0]?.status).toBe('accepted')
    expect((await api.db.select().from(pg.shift).where(eq(pg.shift.id, 'sh-1')))[0]?.status).toBe('closed')
    expect((await push([closed]).expect(201)).body.results[0]?.status).toBe('duplicate')
    const changed = { ...closed, row: { ...closed.row, closedAt: '2026-09-22T13:00:00.000Z' } }
    expect((await push([changed]).expect(201)).body.results[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'rejected', reason: 'CONFLICT' })
  })

  it('a :voided key changes only the status and voidedAt, nothing else on the bill (R-2)', async () => {
    const doc = orderDoc({ orderId: 'o-void', suffix: 'V' })
    await push(doc).expect(201)
    const before = (await api.db.select().from(pg.order).where(eq(pg.order.id, 'o-void')))[0]
    const voided = {
      idempotencyKey: 'order:o-void:voided',
      table: 'order',
      // What a restored tablet really sends: the whole row from its own database, where shiftId is null (Task 10).
      row: { ...(doc[1]!.row as object), status: 'voided', voidedAt: '2026-09-22T09:00:00.000Z', shiftId: null, receiptNo: 'X-999999', queueNo: 999, businessDate: '2020-01-01' },
    }
    expect((await push([voided]).expect(201)).body.results[0]?.status).toBe('accepted')
    const after = (await api.db.select().from(pg.order).where(eq(pg.order.id, 'o-void')))[0]
    expect(after?.status).toBe('voided')
    expect(after?.voidedAt).toBe('2026-09-22T09:00:00.000Z')
    expect(after?.shiftId).toBe(before?.shiftId)            // not erased
    expect(after?.receiptNo).toBe(before?.receiptNo)
    expect(after?.queueNo).toBe(before?.queueNo)
    expect(after?.businessDate).toBe(before?.businessDate)
  })

  it('a :closed key cannot rewrite the opening float (R-2)', async () => {
    const doc = orderDoc({ orderId: 'o-float', shiftId: 'sh-float', suffix: 'F' })
    await push([doc[0]!]).expect(201)
    const before = (await api.db.select().from(pg.shift).where(eq(pg.shift.id, 'sh-float')))[0]
    const closed = {
      idempotencyKey: 'shift:sh-float:closed',
      table: 'shift',
      row: { ...(doc[0]!.row as object), status: 'closed', closedBy: 'u-1', closedAt: '2026-09-22T12:00:00.000Z', openingFloatSatang: 1 },
    }
    expect((await push([closed]).expect(201)).body.results[0]?.status).toBe('accepted')
    const after = (await api.db.select().from(pg.shift).where(eq(pg.shift.id, 'sh-float')))[0]
    expect(after?.status).toBe('closed')
    expect(after?.closedAt).toBe('2026-09-22T12:00:00.000Z')
    expect(after?.openingFloatSatang).toBe(before?.openingFloatSatang)
  })

  it('refuses a state change that goes backwards', async () => {
    const reopened = { idempotencyKey: 'shift:sh-1:closed', table: 'shift', row: { id: 'sh-1', deviceId: auth.deviceId, businessDate: '2026-09-22', status: 'open', openedBy: 'u-1', openedAt: AT, openingFloatSatang: 100_000, closedBy: null, closedAt: null } }
    const res = await push([reopened]).expect(201)
    expect(res.body.results[0]?.status).toBe('rejected')
    expect((await api.db.select().from(pg.shift).where(eq(pg.shift.id, 'sh-1')))[0]?.status).toBe('closed')
  })

  it('refuses a suffix the table is not allowed to use', async () => {
    const [, order] = orderDoc({ orderId: 'o-key' })
    const res = await push([{ ...order!, idempotencyKey: 'order:o-key:closed' }]).expect(201)
    expect(res.body.results[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'rejected', reason: 'BAD_KEY' })
    expect(await api.db.select().from(pg.order).where(eq(pg.order.id, 'o-key'))).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('kills one bad row without blocking the good rows around it (spec §6.1)', async () => {
    const doc = orderDoc({ orderId: 'o-2', suffix: '2' })
    const bad = { idempotencyKey: 'payment:pay-bad', table: 'payment', row: { id: 'pay-bad', orderId: 'o-2', method: 'CASH', amountSatang: 'หกพัน', tenderedSatang: null, changeSatang: null, reference: null, verifyStatus: 'manual', createdBy: 'u-1', createdAt: AT } }
    const results = Object.fromEntries(PushResponse.parse((await push([...doc, bad]).expect(201)).body).results.map((r) => [r.idempotencyKey, r]))
    expect(results['payment:pay-bad']).toMatchObject({ status: 'rejected', reason: 'SCHEMA' })
    expect(results['order:o-2']?.status).toBe('accepted')
    expect(await api.db.select().from(pg.payment).where(eq(pg.payment.id, 'pay-bad'))).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('rejects a row that points at a reference row the server does not have', async () => {
    const doc = orderDoc({ orderId: 'o-3', suffix: '3' })
    const order = { ...doc[1]!, row: { ...(doc[1]!.row as object), channelId: 'ch-missing' } }
    const res = await push([order]).expect(201)
    expect(res.body.results[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'rejected', reason: 'FK_MISSING' })
    expect(await api.db.select().from(pg.order).where(eq(pg.order.id, 'o-3'))).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('defers a child whose transaction parent has not arrived yet', async () => {
    const line = { idempotencyKey: 'order_line:ol-9', table: 'order_line', row: { id: 'ol-9', orderId: 'o-not-yet', lineNo: 1, variantId: 'pv-1', sweetnessId: 'sw-1', recipeId: null, productName: 'ชาไทย', sizeName: '16 oz', sweetnessName: 'หวาน 50%', unitPriceSatang: 6500, qty: 1, lineTotalSatang: 6500, unitCostSatang: 1800 } }
    const res = await push([line]).expect(201)
    expect(res.body.results[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'deferred', reason: 'PARENT_PENDING' })
    expect(await api.db.select().from(pg.orderLine).where(eq(pg.orderLine.id, 'ol-9'))).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('accepts the same deferred row once its parent arrives', async () => {
    const doc = orderDoc({ orderId: 'o-not-yet', suffix: '9' })
    await push([doc[0]!, doc[1]!]).expect(201)
    const line = { idempotencyKey: 'order_line:ol-9', table: 'order_line', row: { id: 'ol-9', orderId: 'o-not-yet', lineNo: 1, variantId: 'pv-1', sweetnessId: 'sw-1', recipeId: null, productName: 'ชาไทย', sizeName: '16 oz', sweetnessName: 'หวาน 50%', unitPriceSatang: 6500, qty: 1, lineTotalSatang: 6500, unitCostSatang: 1800 } }
    expect((await push([line]).expect(201)).body.results[0]?.status).toBe('accepted')
  })

  it('rejects rows that claim to come from another device', async () => {
    const doc = orderDoc({ orderId: 'o-4', deviceId: 'dev-other', suffix: '4' })
    const res = await push([doc[1]!]).expect(201)
    expect(res.body.results[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'rejected', reason: 'NOT_MY_DEVICE' })
  })

  it('refuses a batch larger than 200 rows', async () => {
    const rows = Array.from({ length: 201 }, (_, n) => ({ idempotencyKey: `order:x${n}`, table: 'order', row: {} }))
    const res = await api.http.post('/sync/push').set(auth.headers).send({ deviceId: auth.deviceId, rows }).expect(400)
    expect(res.body.error).toBe('SCHEMA')
  })

  it('refuses to write at all when this process is not the single writer (T5-3)', async () => {
    api.writer.forceForTests(false)
    await push(orderDoc({ orderId: 'o-lock', suffix: 'L' })).expect(503)
    api.writer.forceForTests(true)
  })

  it('refuses a bill that re-uses a queue number instead of failing the whole batch (N-1)', async () => {
    const first = orderDoc({ orderId: 'o-q1', suffix: 'Q1' })
    await push(first).expect(201)
    const clash = orderDoc({ orderId: 'o-q2', suffix: 'Q2' })
    const order = { ...clash[1]!, row: { ...(clash[1]!.row as object), queueNo: (first[1]!.row as { queueNo: number }).queueNo } }
    const res = await push([order, clash[2]!]).expect(201)          // must be 201, never 500
    expect(res.body.results[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'rejected', reason: 'CONFLICT' })
    expect(await api.db.select().from(pg.order).where(eq(pg.order.id, 'o-q2'))).toHaveLength(0)
    expect(res.body.results[1]?.status).toBe('deferred')            // its parent never landed — not a 500 either
  })

  it('refuses a row the database check constraint rejects, as SCHEMA (N-1)', async () => {
    // zod allows it (both are non-negative integers) but order_discount_le_subtotal_ck does not.
    const doc = orderDoc({ orderId: 'o-chk', suffix: 'CK' })
    const bad = { ...doc[1]!, row: { ...(doc[1]!.row as object), subtotalSatang: 100, discountSatang: 900, totalSatang: 0 } }
    const res = await push([doc[0]!, bad]).expect(201)
    expect(res.body.results[1]).toMatchObject({ status: 'rejected', reason: 'SCHEMA' })
    expect(await api.db.select().from(pg.order).where(eq(pg.order.id, 'o-chk'))).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('keeps serving the rest of the queue after a poisoned row (the whole point of N-1)', async () => {
    const good = orderDoc({ orderId: 'o-after', suffix: 'AF' })
    const res = await push(good).expect(201)
    expect(res.body.results.every((r: { status: string }) => r.status === 'accepted' || r.status === 'duplicate')).toBe(true)
  })

  it('rejects a json value that cannot be hashed instead of failing the batch (R-i1)', async () => {
    // zod types payloadJson as unknown, and JSON.parse('{"a":1e999}') really produces Infinity — which
    // canonicalJson refuses. That happens before the transaction, so the rule has to cover the whole function.
    //
    // F-i1 — the body MUST be sent as a raw string. `supertest.send(object)` runs JSON.stringify, and
    // `JSON.stringify({ a: Infinity })` is `'{"a":null}'`: the server would receive a perfectly hashable null and
    // accept the row, making this test fail while the code is right. `1e999` has to travel as text.
    // Do not "simplify" this back into .send({ ... }).
    const doc = orderDoc({ orderId: 'o-inf', suffix: 'IN' })
    await push([doc[0]!, doc[1]!]).expect(201)                            // shift + order first
    const event = doc[4]!.row as Record<string, unknown>
    const body = `{"deviceId":${JSON.stringify(auth.deviceId)},"rows":[{"idempotencyKey":${JSON.stringify(doc[4]!.idempotencyKey)},"table":"order_event","row":${
      JSON.stringify(event).replace(/"payloadJson":\{[^}]*\}/, '"payloadJson":{"total":1e999}')
    }}]}`
    const res = await api.http.post('/sync/push').set(auth.headers).set('content-type', 'application/json')
      .send(body).expect(201)                                             // 201, not 500
    expect(res.body.results[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'rejected', reason: 'SCHEMA' })
    expect(await api.db.select().from(pg.orderEvent).where(eq(pg.orderEvent.id, String(event['id'])))).toHaveLength(0)
  })
})
```

แก้ `apps/api/test/helpers/api-harness.ts` ให้ `createTestApi` รับตัวเลือกหนึ่งตัว:

```ts
export type TestApiOptions = {
  /** 'passthrough' replaces the row verifier with one that accepts everything — for tests of the ingest layer
   *  alone. Leave it out to run against whatever the modules really wire up (Task 7 onwards). */
  verifier?: 'passthrough'
}

// inside createTestApi(options: TestApiOptions = {}), after the other overrideProvider calls:
const builder = Test.createTestingModule({ imports: [AppModule] })
  .overrideProvider(CONFIG).useValue(TEST_CONFIG)
  .overrideProvider(DB).useValue(db)
  .overrideProvider(WRITE_QUEUE).useValue(createWriteQueue())
  .overrideProvider(SINGLE_WRITER).useValue(writer)
if (options.verifier === 'passthrough') {
  builder.overrideProvider(ROW_VERIFIER).useValue({ verifyRow: async () => null, rebuildCostStates: async () => undefined })
}
const moduleRef = await builder.compile()
```

`api.writer` คือ `SingleWriterState` ที่ harness สร้าง (`forceForTests(true)`) — ใช้ทดสอบทางเดินของ 503 เท่านั้น ไม่ใช่ตัว lock
