- [ ] **Step 4: ตารางและความสัมพันธ์ (`apps/api/src/sync/tables.ts`)**

```ts
import type { PgTable } from 'drizzle-orm/pg-core'
import { pg as s } from '@dayo/db-schema'
import type { SyncTable } from '@dayo/contracts'

export const PG_TABLE_OF: Record<SyncTable, PgTable> = {
  shift: s.shift, order: s.order, order_line: s.orderLine, discount: s.discount, payment: s.payment,
  order_event: s.orderEvent, cash_movement: s.cashMovement, cash_count: s.cashCount, z_report: s.zReport,
  purchase: s.purchase, purchase_line: s.purchaseLine, production_batch: s.productionBatch,
  stock_count: s.stockCount, stock_count_line: s.stockCountLine, stock_adjustment: s.stockAdjustment,
  stock_movement: s.stockMovement,
}

/** Which transaction row must already exist before a child can be stored. A missing parent is `deferred`
 *  (it is on its way), unlike a missing reference row which is `rejected` (FK_MISSING). */
export const PARENT_OF: Partial<Record<SyncTable, { table: SyncTable; field: string }>> = {
  order: { table: 'shift', field: 'shiftId' },
  order_line: { table: 'order', field: 'orderId' },
  discount: { table: 'order', field: 'orderId' },
  payment: { table: 'order', field: 'orderId' },
  order_event: { table: 'order', field: 'orderId' },
  cash_movement: { table: 'shift', field: 'shiftId' },
  cash_count: { table: 'shift', field: 'shiftId' },
  z_report: { table: 'shift', field: 'shiftId' },
  purchase_line: { table: 'purchase', field: 'purchaseId' },
  stock_count_line: { table: 'stock_count', field: 'countId' },
}

/** The device id a row carries, when it has one: a device may only push its own rows. */
export const DEVICE_FIELD_OF: Partial<Record<SyncTable, string>> = {
  shift: 'deviceId', order: 'deviceId', order_event: 'deviceId', stock_movement: 'deviceId',
  purchase: 'deviceId', production_batch: 'deviceId', stock_count: 'deviceId', stock_adjustment: 'deviceId',
}

/** Tables whose rows can be updated by a suffixed key, and the column that must move forward (T5-4). */
export const STATUS_FIELD_OF: Record<'order' | 'shift' | 'stock_count', 'status'> = { order: 'status', shift: 'status', stock_count: 'status' }

/**
 * R-2: exactly which columns each allowed transition may write. Everything else on the row keeps the value the
 * server already has — voiding a bill changes the bill's state, not its receipt number, its shift or its date.
 */
export const TRANSITION_FIELDS: Record<'order' | 'shift' | 'stock_count', readonly string[]> = {
  order: ['status', 'voidedAt'],
  shift: ['status', 'closedBy', 'closedAt'],
  stock_count: ['status', 'closedBy', 'closedAt'],
}

/** Which order a row belongs to, for the bill-level re-check of Task 7. */
export function orderIdOf(table: SyncTable, row: Record<string, unknown>): string | null {
  if (table === 'order') return String(row['id'])
  if (table === 'order_line' || table === 'payment' || table === 'discount') return String(row['orderId'])
  return null
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `apps/api/src/sync/push.service.ts`**

```ts
import { Inject, Injectable, Logger, ServiceUnavailableException } from '@nestjs/common'
import { eq } from 'drizzle-orm'
import { pg as s } from '@dayo/db-schema'
import {
  ALLOWED_KEY_SUFFIX, parseIdempotencyKey, SYNC_ROW_SCHEMAS,
  type PushRequest, type PushResponse, type RowResult, type SyncTable,
} from '@dayo/contracts'
import { canonicalJson, sha256Hex, statusTransitionAllowed } from '@dayo/domain'
import { SingleWriterState } from '../db/single-writer.js'
import { DB, SINGLE_WRITER, WRITE_QUEUE, type Db, type WriteQueue } from '../db/types.js'
import { DEVICE_FIELD_OF, PARENT_OF, PG_TABLE_OF, STATUS_FIELD_OF, TRANSITION_FIELDS } from './tables.js'

export type Verdict = Omit<RowResult, 'idempotencyKey'>

/** Thrown inside the row transaction so a failed check rolls back everything it wrote (C-2). */
export class RowRejected extends Error {
  constructor(readonly verdict: Verdict) { super(verdict.reason ?? 'rejected') }
}

type Tx = Parameters<Parameters<Db['transaction']>[0]>[0]

/**
 * N-1: a database error caused by the CONTENT of one row must become that row's verdict. If it escapes, the whole
 * 200-row batch answers 500, and because pushOnce sends FIFO the poisoned row sits at the head of the queue for
 * ever — the tablet would never sync again while the screen says only "cannot reach the server".
 * Anything not in this table is a system failure (cannot connect, deadlock, out of memory) and is allowed to throw.
 */
const ROW_ERROR_VERDICT: Record<string, { reason: Verdict['reason']; what: string }> = {
  '23503': { reason: 'FK_MISSING', what: 'a row it points at does not exist' },       // foreign_key_violation
  '23505': { reason: 'CONFLICT', what: 'it collides with a row already stored' },     // unique_violation
  '23514': { reason: 'SCHEMA', what: 'the database refused the values' },             // check_violation
  '23502': { reason: 'SCHEMA', what: 'a required column was null' },                  // not_null_violation
  '22001': { reason: 'SCHEMA', what: 'a text value is too long' },                    // string_data_right_truncation
  '22P02': { reason: 'SCHEMA', what: 'a value has the wrong type' },                  // invalid_text_representation
}

/**
 * Errors thrown because the row's own content cannot be handled — `canonicalJson` refuses non-finite numbers and
 * bigints (`packages/domain/src/hash.ts`). Matched on the message on purpose, so an ordinary programming bug of the
 * same JavaScript class stays a 500 that somebody notices (F-m2). Widen only with a matching test.
 */
const UNUSABLE_CONTENT = /^canonicalJson:/

/** Walks the whole cause chain (R-i3): drizzle wraps the pg error, and a future version may wrap it twice. */
function pgErrorCode(e: unknown): string | undefined {
  let cur: unknown = e
  for (let depth = 0; cur !== null && cur !== undefined && depth < 5; depth++) {
    const code = (cur as { code?: unknown }).code
    if (typeof code === 'string') return code
    cur = (cur as { cause?: unknown }).cause
  }
  return undefined
}

/** Task 6 registers a provider that throws; Task 7 replaces it with the real one (N-i2). */
export const ROW_VERIFIER = Symbol('ROW_VERIFIER')

export type RowVerifier = {
  verifyRow: (tx: Tx, table: SyncTable, row: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<Verdict | null>
  rebuildCostStates: (tx: Tx, itemIds: string[]) => Promise<void>
}

@Injectable()
export class PushService {
  private readonly log = new Logger(PushService.name)

  constructor(
    @Inject(DB) private readonly db: Db,
    @Inject(WRITE_QUEUE) private readonly write: WriteQueue,
    @Inject(SINGLE_WRITER) private readonly writer: SingleWriterState,
    @Inject(ROW_VERIFIER) private readonly verifier: RowVerifier,
  ) {}

  /**
   * spec §6.1: insert-if-absent per row, answer per row. One bad row never blocks the rows around it, and a row the
   * server refuses is never left behind in the database (C-2). Every write runs inside the single-writer queue so
   * `server_seq` becomes visible in order and a pull can never skip a row that is still being written (T5-3).
   */
  async ingest(deviceId: string, req: PushRequest): Promise<PushResponse> {
    // Proven right here, not by a timer: see SingleWriterState (R-1). One query, on a path that queries anyway.
    if (!(await this.writer.ensureForWrite())) throw new ServiceUnavailableException({ error: 'NOT_WRITER' })
    const results: RowResult[] = []
    const touchedItems = new Set<string>()
    await this.write(async () => {
      for (const pushed of req.rows) {
        const verdict = await this.ingestRow(deviceId, pushed.idempotencyKey, pushed.table, pushed.row)
        results.push({ idempotencyKey: pushed.idempotencyKey, ...verdict })
        // `duplicate` counts too (R-i2): if a batch died halfway after its movements were committed, the re-send
        // answers `duplicate` for every row — and without this the cost cache would silently stay stale for ever.
        if ((verdict.status === 'accepted' || verdict.status === 'duplicate') && pushed.table === 'stock_movement') {
          touchedItems.add(String((pushed.row as Record<string, unknown>)['itemId']))
        }
      }
      // N-m6: rebuild each touched item's cost cache once per batch, not once per movement row. Its own
      // transaction is correct because it reads only movements that are already committed, and the cache is
      // derived data — a later rejected row rolls back its own movement before this ever sees it.
      if (touchedItems.size > 0) {
        await this.db.transaction(async (tx) => { await this.verifier.rebuildCostStates(tx, [...touchedItems]) })
      }
    })
    return { serverTime: new Date().toISOString(), results }
  }

  /**
   * R-i1: the WHOLE row path lives under one rule — anything that throws because of the content of this row
   * becomes this row's verdict, never a 500 for the batch. That includes the code before the transaction:
   * `canonicalJson` throws on a non-finite number, and the four json columns are `z.unknown()` in the contract,
   * so `{"a":1e999}` is a value the device can really queue. Put new code inside `ingestRowOrThrow`, never here.
   */
  private async ingestRow(deviceId: string, key: string, table: SyncTable, raw: unknown): Promise<Verdict> {
    try {
      return await this.ingestRowOrThrow(deviceId, key, table, raw)
    } catch (e) {
      if (e instanceof RowRejected) return e.verdict
      const mapped = ROW_ERROR_VERDICT[pgErrorCode(e) ?? '']
      if (mapped !== undefined) {                                  // N-1: never let one row fail the whole batch
        this.log.warn(`ingest ${key}: ${mapped.what}`)
        return { status: 'rejected', reason: mapped.reason, detail: `${mapped.what}: ${String((e as Error).message).slice(0, 150)}` }
      }
      // F-m2: only the errors that are demonstrably about THIS ROW's content become a verdict. A plain programming
      // bug (reading a property of undefined) is also a TypeError, and turning that into a quiet dead letter would
      // hide it — everywhere else this plan chooses to be loud, so it does here too.
      if (UNUSABLE_CONTENT.test(String((e as Error).message))) {
        this.log.warn(`ingest ${key}: unusable content (${(e as Error).message})`)
        return { status: 'rejected', reason: 'SCHEMA', detail: String((e as Error).message).slice(0, 200) }
      }
      this.log.error(`ingest ${key} failed`, e as Error)
      throw e                                                      // system failure only (cannot connect, deadlock…)
    }
  }

  private async ingestRowOrThrow(deviceId: string, key: string, table: SyncTable, raw: unknown): Promise<Verdict> {
    const parsedKey = parseIdempotencyKey(key)
    if (parsedKey === null || parsedKey.table !== table) return { status: 'rejected', reason: 'BAD_KEY', detail: key }
    if (parsedKey.suffix !== null && parsedKey.suffix !== ALLOWED_KEY_SUFFIX[table]) {
      return { status: 'rejected', reason: 'BAD_KEY', detail: `${table} may not use the suffix ${parsedKey.suffix}` }
    }

    const parsed = SYNC_ROW_SCHEMAS[table].safeParse(raw)
    if (!parsed.success) {
      const issue = parsed.error.issues[0]
      return { status: 'rejected', reason: 'SCHEMA', detail: `${issue?.path.join('.') ?? '(root)'}: ${issue?.message ?? 'invalid'}` }
    }
    const row = parsed.data as Record<string, unknown> & { id: string }
    if (row.id !== parsedKey.id) return { status: 'rejected', reason: 'BAD_KEY', detail: `key says ${parsedKey.id}, row says ${row.id}` }

    const deviceField = DEVICE_FIELD_OF[table]
    if (deviceField !== undefined) {
      const owner = row[deviceField]
      if (owner !== null && owner !== deviceId) return { status: 'rejected', reason: 'NOT_MY_DEVICE', detail: String(owner) }
    }

    const contentHash = sha256Hex(canonicalJson(row))
    const seen = await this.db.select().from(s.idempotencyRecord).where(eq(s.idempotencyRecord.key, key)).limit(1)
    const before = seen[0]
    if (before !== undefined) {
      return before.resultHash === contentHash
        ? { status: 'duplicate' }
        : { status: 'rejected', reason: 'CONFLICT', detail: `${key} was already stored with different content` }
    }

    await this.db.transaction(async (tx) => {
      await this.storeRow(tx, parsedKey.suffix, table, row)
      await tx.insert(s.idempotencyRecord).values({ key, firstSeenAt: new Date().toISOString(), resultHash: contentHash })
      const verdict = await this.verifier.verifyRow(tx, table, row)
      if (verdict !== null) throw new RowRejected(verdict)        // rolls the whole row back (C-2)
    })
    return { status: 'accepted' }
  }

  /** Insert-if-absent, or the one allowed forward state change when the key carries its suffix (C-3 · T5-4). */
  private async storeRow(tx: Tx, suffix: string | null, table: SyncTable, row: Record<string, unknown> & { id: string }): Promise<void> {
    const parent = PARENT_OF[table]
    if (parent !== undefined) {
      const parentId = row[parent.field]
      if (typeof parentId === 'string') {
        const exists = await tx.select({ id: (PG_TABLE_OF[parent.table] as unknown as { id: never }).id })
          .from(PG_TABLE_OF[parent.table]).where(eq((PG_TABLE_OF[parent.table] as unknown as { id: never }).id, parentId)).limit(1)
        if (exists.length === 0) throw new RowRejected({ status: 'deferred', reason: 'PARENT_PENDING', detail: `${parent.table}:${parentId}` })
      }
    }

    const target = PG_TABLE_OF[table]
    const idColumn = (target as unknown as { id: never }).id

    if (suffix === null) {
      await tx.insert(target).values(row as never).onConflictDoNothing()
      return
    }

    // A suffixed key is the device's one allowed update (order paid→voided · shift/stock_count open→closed).
    const statusTable = table as 'order' | 'shift' | 'stock_count'
    const current = await tx.select().from(target).where(eq(idColumn, row.id)).limit(1)
    const existing = current[0] as Record<string, unknown> | undefined
    if (existing === undefined) throw new RowRejected({ status: 'deferred', reason: 'PARENT_PENDING', detail: `${table}:${row.id} has not been stored yet` })
    const field = STATUS_FIELD_OF[statusTable]
    if (!statusTransitionAllowed(statusTable, String(existing[field]), String(row[field]))) {
      throw new RowRejected({ status: 'rejected', reason: 'BAD_TRANSITION', detail: `${String(existing[field])} → ${String(row[field])}` })
    }
    // R-2: write ONLY the columns this transition is allowed to change. `set(row)` would be last-write-wins on the
    // whole bill, which spec §6.1 forbids and Q5-13 promises the owner will not happen — and it is not theoretical:
    // a restored tablet stores old bills with `shiftId: null` (see Task 10), so voiding one of them would quietly
    // erase `shift_id` on the server, breaking invariant 7 of plan 8 and the shift reports of plan 7.
    const patch = Object.fromEntries(TRANSITION_FIELDS[statusTable].map((f) => [f, row[f]]))
    await tx.update(target).set(patch as never).where(eq(idColumn, row.id))
  }
}
```

```ts
import { Body, Controller, Inject, Post, UseGuards } from '@nestjs/common'
import { PushRequest, type PushResponse } from '@dayo/contracts'
import type { DeviceIdentity } from '../auth/auth.service.js'
import { CurrentDevice, DeviceGuard } from '../auth/device.guard.js'
import { parseOrThrow } from '../zod.pipe.js'
import { PushService } from './push.service.js'

@Controller('sync')
@UseGuards(DeviceGuard)
export class SyncController {
  constructor(@Inject(PushService) private readonly push: PushService) {}

  @Post('push')
  async pushRows(@CurrentDevice() device: DeviceIdentity, @Body() body: unknown): Promise<PushResponse> {
    return this.push.ingest(device.deviceId, parseOrThrow(PushRequest, body))
  }
}
```

`apps/api/src/sync/sync.module.ts` — **ค่าเริ่มต้นของตัวตรวจซ้ำคือ "โยน" ไม่ใช่ "ปล่อยผ่าน"** (N-i2): ถ้าวันหนึ่งมีใครประกอบโมดูลใหม่แล้วลืมต่อ `VerifyService` ระบบต้องหยุด ไม่ใช่รับเงินเข้าฐานข้อมูลโดยไม่ตรวจอย่างเงียบ ๆ

```ts
import { Module } from '@nestjs/common'
import { AuthModule } from '../auth/auth.module.js'
import { PushService, ROW_VERIFIER, type RowVerifier } from './push.service.js'
import { SyncController } from './sync.controller.js'

/** Replaced by VerifyService in Task 7. Fails loudly so "no verification" can never be the quiet default. */
const REFUSING_VERIFIER: RowVerifier = {
  verifyRow: () => { throw new Error('no row verifier installed — refusing to accept unverified rows') },
  rebuildCostStates: () => { throw new Error('no row verifier installed — refusing to rebuild cost state') },
}

@Module({
  imports: [AuthModule],
  controllers: [SyncController],
  providers: [PushService, { provide: ROW_VERIFIER, useValue: REFUSING_VERIFIER }],
})
export class SyncModule {}
```

```ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { desc, eq } from 'drizzle-orm'
import { computeEventHash, GENESIS_HASH } from '@dayo/domain'
import { pg } from '@dayo/db-schema'
import { createTestApi, registerTestDevice, type TestApi } from './helpers/api-harness.js'
import { AT, orderDoc, seedReferenceRows } from './helpers/rows.js'

let api: TestApi
let auth: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof registerTestDevice>>
beforeAll(async () => {
  api = await createTestApi()
  auth = await registerTestDevice(api)
  await seedReferenceRows(api.db)
})
afterAll(async () => { await api.close() })

const push = (rows: unknown[]) => api.http.post('/sync/push').set(auth.headers).send({ deviceId: auth.deviceId, rows })

describe('the server re-checks every amount it receives (spec §7)', () => {
  it('kills a bill whose payment does not cover the total', async () => {
    const doc = orderDoc({ orderId: 'o-10', suffix: 'A' })
    const payment = { ...doc[3]!, row: { ...(doc[3]!.row as object), amountSatang: 6400 } }
    const res = await push([doc[0]!, doc[1]!, doc[2]!, payment]).expect(201)
    const last = res.body.results.at(-1)
    expect(last).toMatchObject({ status: 'rejected', reason: 'TOTALS' })
    expect(String(last.detail)).toContain('sum(payment) 6400 != total 6500')
  })

  it('does not keep the row it rejected (C-2)', async () => {
    expect(await api.db.select().from(pg.payment).where(eq(pg.payment.id, 'payA-1'))).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('does not record the key either, so sending it again is still a rejection, not a duplicate', async () => {
    const doc = orderDoc({ orderId: 'o-10', suffix: 'A' })
    const payment = { ...doc[3]!, row: { ...(doc[3]!.row as object), amountSatang: 6400 } }
    expect((await push([payment]).expect(201)).body.results[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'rejected', reason: 'TOTALS' })
  })

  it('accepts the corrected row afterwards', async () => {
    const doc = orderDoc({ orderId: 'o-10', suffix: 'A' })
    expect((await push([doc[3]!]).expect(201)).body.results[0]?.status).toBe('accepted')
  })

  it('kills a bill whose lines do not add up to the subtotal', async () => {
    const doc = orderDoc({ orderId: 'o-11', suffix: 'B' })
    const line = { ...doc[2]!, row: { ...(doc[2]!.row as object), lineTotalSatang: 6400 } }
    const res = await push([doc[0]!, doc[1]!, line, doc[3]!]).expect(201)
    expect(res.body.results.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ status: 'rejected', reason: 'TOTALS' })
  })

  it('accepts a bill that arrives split across two pushes', async () => {
    const doc = orderDoc({ orderId: 'o-12', suffix: 'C' })
    await push([doc[0]!, doc[1]!]).expect(201)
    const res = await push(doc.slice(2)).expect(201)
    expect(res.body.results.every((r: { status: string }) => r.status === 'accepted')).toBe(true)
  })

  it('kills an event whose hash does not match its content (one byte changed)', async () => {
    const tip = (await api.db.select().from(pg.orderEvent).orderBy(desc(pg.orderEvent.chainSeq)).limit(1))[0]
    const core = { orderId: 'o-12', seq: 2, deviceId: auth.deviceId, chainId: auth.deviceId, chainSeq: (tip?.chainSeq ?? 0) + 1, type: 'READY' as const, payload: {}, actorType: 'user' as const, actorId: 'u-1', at: AT }
    const row = { id: 'ev-bad', orderId: core.orderId, seq: core.seq, deviceId: core.deviceId, chainId: core.chainId, chainSeq: core.chainSeq, type: core.type, payloadJson: {}, actorType: core.actorType, actorId: core.actorId, at: core.at, prevHash: tip?.hash ?? GENESIS_HASH, hash: computeEventHash(tip?.hash ?? GENESIS_HASH, { ...core, actorId: 'u-2' }) }
    const res = await push([{ idempotencyKey: 'order_event:ev-bad', table: 'order_event', row }]).expect(201)
    expect(res.body.results[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'rejected', reason: 'CHAIN_BROKEN' })
    expect(await api.db.select().from(pg.orderEvent).where(eq(pg.orderEvent.id, 'ev-bad'))).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('accepts an honest next link (proving the payload mapping is right — C-1)', async () => {
    const tip = (await api.db.select().from(pg.orderEvent).orderBy(desc(pg.orderEvent.chainSeq)).limit(1))[0]
    const core = { orderId: 'o-12', seq: 2, deviceId: auth.deviceId, chainId: auth.deviceId, chainSeq: (tip?.chainSeq ?? 0) + 1, type: 'READY' as const, payload: { note: 'พร้อมรับ' }, actorType: 'user' as const, actorId: 'u-1', at: AT }
    const hash = computeEventHash(tip?.hash ?? GENESIS_HASH, core)
    const row = { id: 'ev-ok', orderId: core.orderId, seq: core.seq, deviceId: core.deviceId, chainId: core.chainId, chainSeq: core.chainSeq, type: core.type, payloadJson: core.payload, actorType: core.actorType, actorId: core.actorId, at: core.at, prevHash: tip?.hash ?? GENESIS_HASH, hash }
    expect((await push([{ idempotencyKey: 'order_event:ev-ok', table: 'order_event', row }]).expect(201)).body.results[0]?.status).toBe('accepted')
  })

  it('defers an event that skips a chain_seq (the earlier one may still arrive)', async () => {
    const core = { orderId: 'o-12', seq: 9, deviceId: auth.deviceId, chainId: auth.deviceId, chainSeq: 99, type: 'READY' as const, payload: {}, actorType: 'user' as const, actorId: 'u-1', at: AT }
    const prev = 'a'.repeat(64)
    const row = { id: 'ev-9', orderId: core.orderId, seq: core.seq, deviceId: core.deviceId, chainId: core.chainId, chainSeq: core.chainSeq, type: core.type, payloadJson: {}, actorType: core.actorType, actorId: core.actorId, at: core.at, prevHash: prev, hash: computeEventHash(prev, core) }
    expect((await push([{ idempotencyKey: 'order_event:ev-9', table: 'order_event', row }]).expect(201)).body.results[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'deferred', reason: 'CHAIN_GAP' })
  })

  it('rebuilds item_cost_state from the movements it stored (the device never pushes the cache)', async () => {
    const rows = await api.db.select().from(pg.itemCostState).where(eq(pg.itemCostState.itemId, 'it-1'))
    expect(rows[0]?.onHandMilli).toBeLessThan(0)          // sales only — negative on hand is allowed (§4.2)
    expect(rows[0]?.avgCostUsat).toBe(19_000_000)         // standard cost until something is received (§4.4)
  })

  it('rebuilds the cost cache again when the same movements come back as duplicates (R-i2)', async () => {
    const doc = orderDoc({ orderId: 'o-dup', suffix: 'DP' })
    await push(doc).expect(201)
    const rows = await api.db.select().from(pg.itemCostState).where(eq(pg.itemCostState.itemId, 'it-1'))
    const onHand = rows[0]?.onHandMilli ?? 0
    // Simulate a batch that died after committing: the cache is stale, the device re-sends, everything is duplicate.
    await api.db.update(pg.itemCostState).set({ onHandMilli: 999_999 }).where(eq(pg.itemCostState.itemId, 'it-1'))
    const again = await push(doc).expect(201)
    expect(again.body.results.every((r: { status: string }) => r.status === 'duplicate')).toBe(true)
    expect((await api.db.select().from(pg.itemCostState).where(eq(pg.itemCostState.itemId, 'it-1')))[0]?.onHandMilli).toBe(onHand)
  })

  it('reaches the same cost state after a receipt, using the domain rebuild (D57 grouping)', async () => {
    const before = (await api.db.select().from(pg.itemCostState).where(eq(pg.itemCostState.itemId, 'it-1')))[0]
    const purchase = [
      { idempotencyKey: 'purchase:pu-1', table: 'purchase', row: { id: 'pu-1', businessDate: '2026-09-22', supplier: 'ร้านส่ง', totalSatang: 7700, receiptImageRef: null, note: null, deviceId: auth.deviceId, createdBy: 'u-1', createdAt: AT } },
      { idempotencyKey: 'purchase_line:pl-1', table: 'purchase_line', row: { id: 'pl-1', purchaseId: 'pu-1', itemId: 'it-1', purchaseUnitId: null, qtyUnitsMilli: 1000, qtyUseMilli: 400_000, lineTotalSatang: 7700 } },
      { idempotencyKey: 'stock_movement:mv-2', table: 'stock_movement', row: { id: 'mv-2', itemId: 'it-1', kind: 'PURCHASE', qtyMilli: 400_000, unitCostUsat: 19_250_000, refType: 'purchase', refId: 'pu-1', businessDate: '2026-09-22', deviceId: auth.deviceId, createdBy: 'u-1', createdAt: AT } },
    ]
    await push(purchase).expect(201)
    const after = (await api.db.select().from(pg.itemCostState).where(eq(pg.itemCostState.itemId, 'it-1')))[0]
    expect(after?.onHandMilli).toBe((before?.onHandMilli ?? 0) + 400_000)
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `apps/api/src/sync/verify.service.ts`**

```ts
import { Injectable } from '@nestjs/common'
import { asc, desc, eq, inArray } from 'drizzle-orm'
import { pg as s } from '@dayo/db-schema'
import type { SyncTable } from '@dayo/contracts'
import { chainedEventFromRow, rebuildCostState, verifyChainLink, verifyOrderTotals } from '@dayo/domain'
import type { Db } from '../db/types.js'
import type { RowVerifier, Verdict } from './push.service.js'

type Tx = Parameters<Parameters<Db['transaction']>[0]>[0]

@Injectable()
export class VerifyService implements RowVerifier {
  /** Every query runs on the caller's `tx`, so this service holds no dependency of its own (N-m2). */

  /** Runs inside the same transaction as the write, so a failed check rolls the row back (C-2). */
  async verifyRow(tx: Tx, table: SyncTable, row: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<Verdict | null> {
    if (table === 'order_event') return this.checkChain(tx, row)
    const orderId = orderIdOf(table, row)
    if (orderId !== null) return this.checkOrderTotals(tx, orderId)
    return null                                   // stock_movement: the cost cache is rebuilt per batch (N-m6)
  }

  /**
   * The event being checked is already inserted by the caller, so the stored tip is the link *before* it — read the
   * highest chain_seq that is smaller than this row's.
   */
  private async checkChain(tx: Tx, row: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<Verdict | null> {
    const chainId = String(row['chainId'])
    const chainSeq = Number(row['chainSeq'])
    const tipRows = await tx
      .select({ chainSeq: s.orderEvent.chainSeq, hash: s.orderEvent.hash })
      .from(s.orderEvent)
      .where(eq(s.orderEvent.chainId, chainId))
      .orderBy(desc(s.orderEvent.chainSeq))
      .limit(2)
    const tip = tipRows.find((r) => r.chainSeq < chainSeq) ?? null
    const verdict = verifyChainLink(tip, chainedEventFromRow(row))    // the shared mapper from @dayo/domain (N-m3)
    if (verdict.ok) return null
    return verdict.kind === 'gap'
      ? { status: 'deferred', reason: 'CHAIN_GAP', detail: verdict.reason }
      : { status: 'rejected', reason: 'CHAIN_BROKEN', detail: verdict.reason }
  }

  /** spec §7 invariant 1, on the rows now in the transaction — so a bill assembled from two pushes is judged once
   *  it is complete, and not before (a bill with no payment row yet is simply not judged on its payments). */
  private async checkOrderTotals(tx: Tx, orderId: string): Promise<Verdict | null> {
    const orders = await tx.select().from(s.order).where(eq(s.order.id, orderId)).limit(1)
    const order = orders[0]
    if (!order) return null
    const lines = await tx.select({ total: s.orderLine.lineTotalSatang }).from(s.orderLine).where(eq(s.orderLine.orderId, orderId))
    if (lines.length === 0) return null                      // the lines have not arrived yet — nothing to check
    const payments = await tx.select({ amount: s.payment.amountSatang }).from(s.payment).where(eq(s.payment.orderId, orderId))
    const verdict = verifyOrderTotals({
      subtotalSatang: order.subtotalSatang, discountSatang: order.discountSatang, totalSatang: order.totalSatang,
      status: payments.length === 0 ? 'pending_payment' : order.status,   // do not judge payments that are still on their way
      lineTotals: lines.map((l) => l.total), paymentAmounts: payments.map((p) => p.amount),
    })
    return verdict.ok ? null : { status: 'rejected', reason: 'TOTALS', detail: verdict.reason }
  }

  /**
   * The device never pushes `item_cost_state` (plan 3 §5): the server rebuilds it from the movements it has, with
   * the same domain function the tablet uses, so the moving average and D57's per-document grouping stay identical.
   */
  async rebuildCostStates(tx: Tx, itemIds: string[]): Promise<void> {
    const items = await tx.select({ id: s.item.id, standard: s.item.standardCostUsat }).from(s.item).where(inArray(s.item.id, itemIds))
    const at = new Date().toISOString()
    for (const item of items) {
      const movements = await tx
        .select({ qtyMilli: s.stockMovement.qtyMilli, unitCostUsat: s.stockMovement.unitCostUsat, refType: s.stockMovement.refType, refId: s.stockMovement.refId })
        .from(s.stockMovement).where(eq(s.stockMovement.itemId, item.id))
        .orderBy(asc(s.stockMovement.createdAt), asc(s.stockMovement.id))
      const state = rebuildCostState(movements, item.standard)
      await tx.insert(s.itemCostState)
        .values({ itemId: item.id, onHandMilli: state.onHandMilli, avgCostUsat: state.avgCostUsat, asOfMovementId: null, updatedAt: at })
        .onConflictDoUpdate({ target: s.itemCostState.itemId, set: { onHandMilli: state.onHandMilli, avgCostUsat: state.avgCostUsat, updatedAt: at } })
    }
  }
}
```

`apps/api/test/pull.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { eq } from 'drizzle-orm'
import { pg } from '@dayo/db-schema'
import { PullResponse } from '@dayo/contracts'
import { createTestApi, registerTestDevice, type TestApi } from './helpers/api-harness.js'
import { AT, orderDoc, seedReferenceRows } from './helpers/rows.js'

let api: TestApi
let auth: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof registerTestDevice>>
beforeAll(async () => {
  api = await createTestApi()
  auth = await registerTestDevice(api)
  await seedReferenceRows(api.db)
})
afterAll(async () => { await api.close() })

const pull = (q: string) => api.http.get(`/sync/pull?${q}`).set(auth.headers)

describe('GET /sync/pull', () => {
  it('refuses a call without a device token', async () => {
    await api.http.get('/sync/pull?table=item&since=0').expect(401)
  })

  it('refuses a table that is not pullable', async () => {
    expect((await pull('table=outbox&since=0').expect(400)).body.error).toBe('SCHEMA')
  })

  it('returns reference rows from the beginning with a next cursor', async () => {
    const body = PullResponse.parse((await pull('table=item&since=0').expect(200)).body)
    expect(body.rows).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(body.nextSince).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(body.hasMore).toBe(false)
  })

  it('returns nothing when the cursor is already up to date', async () => {
    const first = PullResponse.parse((await pull('table=item&since=0').expect(200)).body)
    const again = PullResponse.parse((await pull(`table=item&since=${first.nextSince}`).expect(200)).body)
    expect(again.rows).toHaveLength(0)
    expect(again.nextSince).toBe(first.nextSince)
  })

  it('shows an edited row again because the bump_server_seq trigger gives it a new sequence', async () => {
    const first = PullResponse.parse((await pull('table=item&since=0').expect(200)).body)
    await api.db.update(pg.item).set({ name: 'ชาไทยผง (ใหม่)', updatedAt: AT }).where(eq(pg.item.id, 'it-1'))
    const after = PullResponse.parse((await pull(`table=item&since=${first.nextSince}`).expect(200)).body)
    expect(after.rows).toHaveLength(1)
    expect((after.rows[0] as { name: string }).name).toBe('ชาไทยผง (ใหม่)')
  })

  it('pages with limit, reports hasMore and never repeats a row', async () => {
    for (let n = 2; n <= 5; n++) {
      await api.db.insert(pg.item).values({ id: `it-${n}`, code: `RM-X-0${n}`, name: `ของ ${n}`, kind: 'raw', category: 'ของดิบ', useUnit: 'g', isTracked: true, reorderPointMilli: 0, standardCostUsat: 1_000_000, shelfLifeHours: null, isActive: true, note: null, updatedAt: AT, version: 1 })
    }
    const page = PullResponse.parse((await pull('table=item&since=0&limit=2').expect(200)).body)
    expect(page.rows).toHaveLength(2)
    expect(page.hasMore).toBe(true)
    const next = PullResponse.parse((await pull(`table=item&since=${page.nextSince}&limit=2`).expect(200)).body)
    const firstIds = page.rows.map((r) => (r as { id: string }).id)
    const nextIds = next.rows.map((r) => (r as { id: string }).id)
    expect(firstIds.some((id) => nextIds.includes(id))).toBe(false)
  })

  it('caps limit at 500', async () => {
    await pull('table=item&since=0&limit=501').expect(400)
  })

  it('answers rows in server_seq order, so a cursor can never skip one', async () => {
    const all = PullResponse.parse((await pull('table=item&since=0&limit=500').expect(200)).body)
    const seqs: number[] = []
    let cursor = 0
    for (const _row of all.rows) {
      const page = PullResponse.parse((await pull(`table=item&since=${cursor}&limit=1`).expect(200)).body)
      seqs.push(page.nextSince)
      cursor = page.nextSince
    }
    expect(seqs).toEqual([...seqs].sort((a, b) => a - b))
  })

  it('pulls the transaction tables of spec §6.1 too (server-written orders and the cost cache)', async () => {
    await api.http.post('/sync/push').set(auth.headers).send({ deviceId: auth.deviceId, rows: orderDoc({ orderId: 'o-20', suffix: 'P' }) }).expect(201)
    const orders = PullResponse.parse((await pull('table=order&since=0').expect(200)).body)
    expect(orders.rows.map((r) => (r as { id: string }).id)).toContain('o-20')
    expect(PullResponse.parse((await pull('table=item_cost_state&since=0').expect(200)).body).rows.length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
  })

  it('never returns the server-only columns the device has no place for', async () => {
    const row = PullResponse.parse((await pull('table=order&since=0').expect(200)).body).rows[0] as Record<string, unknown>
    expect(row['serverSeq']).toBeUndefined()
    expect(row['serverReceivedAt']).toBeUndefined()
  })
})

describe('the 30-day history window (spec §6.4 · Q5-11 · I-4)', () => {
  beforeAll(async () => {
    // An old bill, outside any sensible window.
    await api.db.insert(pg.order).values({ id: 'o-old', origin: 'device', deviceId: auth.deviceId, receiptNo: 'A-000099', queueNo: 9, businessDate: '2026-01-05', shiftId: null, channelId: 'ch-1', customerId: null, status: 'paid', subtotalSatang: 100, discountSatang: 0, totalSatang: 100, vatSatang: 0, costSatang: 0, note: null, createdByType: 'user', createdById: 'u-1', createdAt: '2026-01-05T03:00:00.000Z', paidAt: '2026-01-05T03:00:00.000Z', readyAt: null, voidedAt: null })
    await api.db.insert(pg.payment).values({ id: 'pay-old', orderId: 'o-old', method: 'CASH', amountSatang: 100, tenderedSatang: 100, changeSatang: 0, reference: null, verifyStatus: 'manual', createdBy: 'u-1', createdAt: '2026-01-05T03:00:00.000Z' })
  })

  it('leaves out bills older than sinceDate', async () => {
    const ids = PullResponse.parse((await pull('table=order&since=0&sinceDate=2026-08-23').expect(200)).body).rows.map((r) => (r as { id: string }).id)
    expect(ids).not.toContain('o-old')
    expect(ids).toContain('o-20')
  })

  it('leaves out the children of those bills as well', async () => {
    const ids = PullResponse.parse((await pull('table=payment&since=0&sinceDate=2026-08-23').expect(200)).body).rows.map((r) => (r as { id: string }).id)
    expect(ids).not.toContain('pay-old')
  })

  it('still returns everything when no window is asked for', async () => {
    const ids = PullResponse.parse((await pull('table=order&since=0').expect(200)).body).rows.map((r) => (r as { id: string }).id)
    expect(ids).toContain('o-old')
  })

  it('refuses a window on a reference table', async () => {
    await pull('table=item&since=0&sinceDate=2026-08-23').expect(400)
  })
})

describe('what a pulled setting means for the device (plan 3b hand-off)', () => {
  it('serves cash.variance_alert_satang so the tablet stops using its built-in default', async () => {
    await api.db.insert(pg.setting).values({ key: 'cash.variance_alert_satang', valueJson: 5_000, effectiveFrom: AT, updatedAt: AT, version: 1 })
    const rows = PullResponse.parse((await pull('table=setting&since=0').expect(200)).body).rows
    expect(rows.some((r) => (r as { key: string }).key === 'cash.variance_alert_satang')).toBe(true)
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `apps/api/src/sync/pull.service.ts`**

```ts
import { Inject, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common'
import { and, asc, getTableColumns, gt, sql, type SQL } from 'drizzle-orm'
import type { PgTable } from 'drizzle-orm/pg-core'
import { pg as s } from '@dayo/db-schema'
import type { PullQuery, PullResponse, PullTable } from '@dayo/contracts'
import { DB, type Db } from '../db/types.js'

export const PULL_TABLE_OF: Record<PullTable, PgTable> = {
  user: s.user, device: s.device, setting: s.setting, category: s.category, item: s.item,
  purchase_unit: s.purchaseUnit, bom: s.bom, bom_line: s.bomLine, product: s.product, size: s.size,
  product_variant: s.productVariant, sweetness_level: s.sweetnessLevel, channel: s.channel, price: s.price,
  recipe: s.recipe, recipe_line: s.recipeLine, equipment: s.equipment, customer: s.customer,
  order: s.order, order_line: s.orderLine, order_event: s.orderEvent, payment: s.payment,
  item_cost_state: s.itemCostState,
}

/** Columns the device has no column for — they exist only on the server side of the schema. */
const SERVER_ONLY = new Set(['serverSeq', 'serverReceivedAt'])

@Injectable()
export class PullService {
  constructor(@Inject(DB) private readonly db: Db) {}

  /**
   * spec §6.1: `GET /sync/pull?table=…&since=server_seq`. A row edited on the server gets a fresh server_seq from
   * the `bump_server_seq` trigger (plan 2), so an edit is pulled again. Reading needs no snapshot guard because
   * this process is the only writer (T5-3) and every write runs inside WriteQueue: by the time a row is visible,
   * every smaller server_seq is committed too.
   */
  async pull(q: PullQuery): Promise<PullResponse> {
    const table = PULL_TABLE_OF[q.table]
    const columns = getTableColumns(table)
    const seq = columns['serverSeq']
    if (seq === undefined) throw new Error(`table ${q.table} has no server_seq`)
    const selection = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(columns).filter(([name]) => !SERVER_ONLY.has(name)))

    const where: SQL[] = [gt(seq, q.since)]
    const window = this.historyWindow(q)
    if (window !== null) where.push(window)

    const rows = await this.db
      .select({ ...selection, __seq: seq })
      .from(table)
      .where(and(...where))
      .orderBy(asc(seq))
      .limit(q.limit + 1)

    const hasMore = rows.length > q.limit
    const page = hasMore ? rows.slice(0, q.limit) : rows
    const last = page.at(-1) as { __seq?: number } | undefined
    return {
      serverTime: new Date().toISOString(),
      table: q.table,
      rows: page.map((r) => { const { __seq: _drop, ...rest } = r as Record<string, unknown> & { __seq: number }; return rest }),
      nextSince: last?.__seq ?? q.since,
      hasMore,
    }
  }

  /** spec §6.4 · Q5-11: a replacement tablet takes the last 30 days of bills, not the whole history (I-4). */
  private historyWindow(q: PullQuery): SQL | null {
    if (q.sinceDate === undefined) return null
    if (q.table === 'order') return sql`${s.order.businessDate} >= ${q.sinceDate}`
    const child = { order_line: s.orderLine, order_event: s.orderEvent, payment: s.payment }[q.table as 'order_line' | 'order_event' | 'payment']
    return sql`exists (select 1 from ${s.order} where ${s.order.id} = ${child.orderId} and ${s.order.businessDate} >= ${q.sinceDate})`
  }
}
```

### Task 9: แท็บเล็ต — transport + ตัวส่ง outbox (sent / dead / deferred) 💰 review: opus

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/pos/src/sync/keys.ts`, `apps/pos/src/sync/transport.ts`, `apps/pos/src/sync/push.ts`, `apps/pos/test/helpers/sync.ts`, `apps/pos/test/sync-push.test.ts`
- Modify: `apps/pos/src/api/deps.ts`, `apps/pos/src/api/errors.ts`, `apps/pos/src/db/worker.ts`, `apps/pos/test/helpers/db.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `RemoteDb` · `s.outbox`, `s.syncState` · `PushRequest/PushResponse`, `MAX_PUSH_ROWS` (contracts) · `ApiDeps` · `localDeviceId` (`api/bootstrap.ts`)
- Produces:
  - `SYNC_KEYS` = `{ baseUrl: 'server.base_url', token: 'server.device_token', lastPushAt: 'sync.last_push_at', lastPullAt: 'sync.last_pull_at', lastServerTime: 'sync.last_server_time', skewMs: 'sync.clock_skew_ms', cursor: (t) => 'sync.cursor.' + t }` · `CLOCK_SKEW_WARN_MS = 300_000` · `MAX_DEFERRED_ATTEMPTS = 10`
  - `createTransport(db, deps): Promise<SyncTransport | null>` · `SyncTransport = { deviceId; push; pull; inboxFlag }` · `class SyncHttpError extends Error { status }`
  - `encodeLastError(reason, detail): string` · `decodeLastError(s): { reason: string; detail: string }` — JSON ไม่ใช่การ split สตริง (I-12)
  - `pushOnce(db, deps, transport): Promise<PushOutcome>` = `{ sent, dead, deferred }`
  - `ApiDeps` เพิ่ม `fetch` · `PosErrorCode` เพิ่ม `SYNC_OFFLINE` · `SYNC_UNAUTHORIZED` · `NOT_REGISTERED` · `ALREADY_REGISTERED`
- เทสต์: `fakeServer()` · `markRegistered(db)` · `TEST_SERVER` · `outboxRows(db, status)` · `openTestApi({ fetch })`

- [ ] **Step 1: ตัวช่วยเทสต์ + เทสต์ให้ตก**

`apps/pos/test/helpers/sync.ts`:

```ts
import { eq } from 'drizzle-orm'
import type { RemoteDb } from '@dayo/db-schema/browser'
import * as s from '@dayo/db-schema/sqlite'
import type { PushRequest, RowResult } from '@dayo/contracts'
import { SYNC_KEYS } from '../../src/sync/keys'

export const TEST_SERVER = 'http://api.test'

export type FakeServer = {
  fetch: typeof globalThis.fetch
  pushes: PushRequest[]
  /** Decide the answer for one row; the default accepts everything. */
  verdict: (row: PushRequest['rows'][number], index: number) => Omit<RowResult, 'idempotencyKey'>
  pullRows: Map<string, { rows: Record<string, unknown>[]; nextSince: number }>
  serverTime: string
  hasInbox: boolean
  offline: boolean
  unauthorized: boolean
  calls: string[]
}

export function fakeServer(): FakeServer {
  const f = {
    pushes: [] as PushRequest[],
    verdict: () => ({ status: 'accepted' as const }),
    pullRows: new Map<string, { rows: Record<string, unknown>[]; nextSince: number }>(),
    serverTime: '2026-09-17T03:00:05.000Z',
    hasInbox: false,
    offline: false,
    unauthorized: false,
    calls: [] as string[],
  } as FakeServer
  const json = (body: unknown, status = 200) => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } })
  f.fetch = (async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {
    const url = new URL(String(input))
    f.calls.push(`${init?.method ?? 'GET'} ${url.pathname}${url.search}`)
    if (f.offline) throw new TypeError('Failed to fetch')
    if (f.unauthorized) return json({ error: 'BAD_TOKEN' }, 401)
    if (url.pathname === '/sync/push') {
      const body = JSON.parse(String(init?.body)) as PushRequest
      f.pushes.push(body)
      return json({ serverTime: f.serverTime, results: body.rows.map((r, i) => ({ idempotencyKey: r.idempotencyKey, ...f.verdict(r, i) })) }, 201)
    }
    if (url.pathname === '/sync/pull') {
      const table = url.searchParams.get('table') ?? ''
      const since = Number(url.searchParams.get('since') ?? '0')
      const prepared = f.pullRows.get(table) ?? { rows: [], nextSince: since }
      return json({ serverTime: f.serverTime, table, rows: prepared.rows, nextSince: prepared.nextSince, hasMore: false })
    }
    if (url.pathname === '/sync/inbox-flag') return json({ hasInbox: f.hasInbox, serverTime: f.serverTime })
    // `writer` is part of HealthResponse, which is a strictObject — leaving it out would fail the moment
    // anything parses this answer (F-m1).
    if (url.pathname === '/health') return json({ ok: true, serverTime: f.serverTime, version: 'test', writer: true })
    return new Response('not found', { status: 404 })
  }) as typeof globalThis.fetch
  return f
}

/** Marks the device as registered so the transport has somewhere to call. */
export async function markRegistered(db: RemoteDb, token = 'token-'.padEnd(40, 'x')): Promise<void> {
  for (const [key, value] of [[SYNC_KEYS.baseUrl, TEST_SERVER], [SYNC_KEYS.token, token]] as const) {
    await db.insert(s.syncState).values({ key, value }).onConflictDoUpdate({ target: s.syncState.key, set: { value } })
  }
}

export async function outboxRows(db: RemoteDb, status: 'pending' | 'sent' | 'dead') {
  return db.select().from(s.outbox).where(eq(s.outbox.status, status)).all()
}
```

`apps/pos/test/sync-push.test.ts`:

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { chainedEventFromRow, GENESIS_HASH, verifyChainLink } from '@dayo/domain'
import { decodeLastError, MAX_DEFERRED_ATTEMPTS } from '../src/sync/keys'
import { pushOnce } from '../src/sync/push'
import { createTransport, SyncHttpError } from '../src/sync/transport'
import { openReadyApi, sellSku, type ReadyApi } from './helpers/db'
import { fakeServer, markRegistered, outboxRows, type FakeServer } from './helpers/sync'

let t: ReadyApi
let server: FakeServer

const CASH = { method: 'CASH' as const, tenderedSatang: 10_000 }

beforeEach(async () => {
  server = fakeServer()
  t = await openReadyApi({ fetch: server.fetch })
  await markRegistered(t.db)
})

/**
 * N-i4: this file drives `pushOnce` directly through the transport it also owns, so Task 9 is green on its own —
 * no `PosApi.syncNow` (that arrives in Task 10, which tests the wiring instead). The database writes still go
 * through the same functions the Worker will call.
 */
async function runPush(): Promise<{ sent: number; dead: number; deferred: number } | 'no-transport'> {
  const transport = await createTransport(t.db, t.deps)
  if (transport === null) return 'no-transport'
  return pushOnce(t.db, t.deps, transport)
}

describe('pushOnce', () => {
  it('sends what openReadyApi already queued (the open shift) and marks it sent', async () => {
    // openReadyApi opens a shift, so the outbox is never empty at the start of a test (I-14).
    expect(await outboxRows(t.db, 'pending')).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(await runPush()).toMatchObject({ sent: 1, dead: 0 })
    expect(await outboxRows(t.db, 'pending')).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('does nothing on a second run when everything is already sent', async () => {
    await runPush()
    server.pushes.length = 0
    expect(await runPush()).toMatchObject({ sent: 0, dead: 0, deferred: 0 })
    expect(server.pushes).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('sends a whole bill and marks the rows sent', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    const run = await runPush()
    expect(run).toMatchObject({ dead: 0, deferred: 0 })
    expect((run as { sent: number }).sent).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(6)   // shift + order + line + payment + event + movement
    expect(await outboxRows(t.db, 'pending')).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('sends rows FIFO so a parent always travels before its children', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    await runPush()
    const keys = server.pushes.flatMap((p) => p.rows.map((r) => r.idempotencyKey))
    expect(keys.findIndex((k) => k.startsWith('order:'))).toBeLessThan(keys.findIndex((k) => k.startsWith('payment:')))
  })

  it('never sends more than 200 rows in one request', async () => {
    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i++) await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    await runPush()
    expect(Math.max(...server.pushes.map((p) => p.rows.length))).toBeLessThanOrEqual(200)
    expect(await outboxRows(t.db, 'pending')).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('moves a rejected row to dead with its reason, and keeps sending the rest (spec §6.1)', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    server.verdict = (r) => (r.table === 'payment' ? { status: 'rejected', reason: 'TOTALS', detail: 'sum(payment) 1 != total 6500' } : { status: 'accepted' })
    await runPush()
    const dead = await outboxRows(t.db, 'dead')
    expect(dead).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(dead[0]?.tableName).toBe('payment')
    expect(dead[0]?.deadAt).not.toBeNull()
    expect(decodeLastError(dead[0]?.lastError ?? '')).toEqual({ reason: 'TOTALS', detail: 'sum(payment) 1 != total 6500' })
    expect(await outboxRows(t.db, 'pending')).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('keeps a detail that contains ": " intact (I-12)', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    server.verdict = (r) => (r.table === 'payment' ? { status: 'rejected', reason: 'SCHEMA', detail: 'amountSatang: expected number' } : { status: 'accepted' })
    await runPush()
    const dead = await outboxRows(t.db, 'dead')
    expect(decodeLastError(dead[0]?.lastError ?? '')).toEqual({ reason: 'SCHEMA', detail: 'amountSatang: expected number' })
  })

  it('leaves a deferred row pending and counts an attempt', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    server.verdict = (r) => (r.table === 'order_event' ? { status: 'deferred', reason: 'CHAIN_GAP', detail: 'expected chain_seq 1, got 2' } : { status: 'accepted' })
    await runPush()
    const pending = await outboxRows(t.db, 'pending')
    expect(pending).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(pending[0]?.attempts).toBe(1)
    expect(pending[0]?.deadAt).toBeNull()
  })

  it('gives up on a row that stays deferred and puts it in the dead list (I-1 · Q5-19)', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    server.verdict = (r) => (r.table === 'order_event' ? { status: 'deferred', reason: 'PARENT_PENDING', detail: 'order:o-1' } : { status: 'accepted' })
    for (let i = 0; i < MAX_DEFERRED_ATTEMPTS; i++) await runPush()
    const dead = await outboxRows(t.db, 'dead')
    expect(dead).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(decodeLastError(dead[0]?.lastError ?? '').reason).toBe('PARENT_PENDING')
    expect(await outboxRows(t.db, 'pending')).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('stops the round instead of re-sending the same deferred batch forever (I-2)', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    server.verdict = () => ({ status: 'deferred', reason: 'PARENT_PENDING', detail: 'x' })
    await runPush()
    expect(server.pushes).toHaveLength(1)                  // one request, not a loop
  })

  it('treats a duplicate as sent (a re-send after a lost answer must settle)', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    server.verdict = () => ({ status: 'duplicate' })
    await runPush()
    expect(await outboxRows(t.db, 'pending')).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('keeps everything pending when the network is down, and kills nothing', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    server.offline = true
    await expect(runPush()).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(TypeError)     // run.ts turns this into SYNC_OFFLINE (Task 10)
    expect((await outboxRows(t.db, 'pending')).length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(await outboxRows(t.db, 'dead')).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('does not kill rows when the server answers 500', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    server.fetch = (async () => new Response('boom', { status: 500 })) as typeof globalThis.fetch
    await expect(runPush()).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(SyncHttpError)
    expect(await outboxRows(t.db, 'dead')).toHaveLength(0)
    expect((await outboxRows(t.db, 'pending')).length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
  })

  it('does not kill rows when the token was revoked (401)', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    server.unauthorized = true
    await expect(runPush()).rejects.toMatchObject({ status: 401 })
    expect(await outboxRows(t.db, 'dead')).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('has no transport at all when the device is not registered yet', async () => {
    const fresh = await openReadyApi({ fetch: server.fetch })       // no markRegistered
    await sellSku(fresh, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    expect(await createTransport(fresh.db, fresh.deps)).toBeNull()
    expect(server.pushes).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('sends this device id on every push', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    await runPush()
    expect(server.pushes[0]?.deviceId).toBe(t.device.id)
  })

  it('sends a real event row whose hash the server-side verifier accepts (C-1 cross-check)', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    await runPush()
    const eventRow = server.pushes.flatMap((p) => p.rows).find((r) => r.table === 'order_event')
    expect(eventRow).toBeDefined()
    // The very mapper apps/api uses (N-m3): if it ever stops matching what the device writes, this fails.
    const chained = chainedEventFromRow(eventRow!.row as Record<string, unknown>)
    expect(chained.prevHash).toBe(GENESIS_HASH)
    expect(verifyChainLink(null, chained)).toEqual({ ok: true })
  })
})
```

`apps/pos/src/sync/keys.ts`:

```ts
/** Every sync_state key this plan owns. All of them are local-only: they never enter the outbox (plan 3 §5). */
export const SYNC_KEYS = {
  baseUrl: 'server.base_url',
  token: 'server.device_token',
  lastPushAt: 'sync.last_push_at',
  lastPullAt: 'sync.last_pull_at',
  lastServerTime: 'sync.last_server_time',
  skewMs: 'sync.clock_skew_ms',
  cursor: (table: string): string => `sync.cursor.${table}`,
} as const

/** spec §6.3: warn when the tablet's clock is more than 5 minutes away from the server's. */
export const CLOCK_SKEW_WARN_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000

/** Q5-19 · I-1: a row that keeps waiting for a parent that never comes must not wait forever in silence. */
export const MAX_DEFERRED_ATTEMPTS = 10

/** outbox.lastError holds JSON, not "REASON: detail" — a detail may contain ': ' itself (I-12). */
export function encodeLastError(reason: string, detail: string): string {
  return JSON.stringify({ reason, detail }).slice(0, 500)
}

export function decodeLastError(raw: string): { reason: string; detail: string } {
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as { reason?: unknown; detail?: unknown }
    return { reason: typeof parsed.reason === 'string' ? parsed.reason : '', detail: typeof parsed.detail === 'string' ? parsed.detail : '' }
  } catch {
    return { reason: '', detail: raw }
  }
}
```

`apps/pos/src/sync/transport.ts`:

```ts
import { eq } from 'drizzle-orm'
import type { RemoteDb } from '@dayo/db-schema/browser'
import * as s from '@dayo/db-schema/sqlite'
import { PullResponse, PushResponse, type InboxFlagResponse, type PullTable, type PushRequest } from '@dayo/contracts'
import { localDeviceId } from '../api/bootstrap'
import type { ApiDeps } from '../api/deps'
import { SYNC_KEYS } from './keys'

export class SyncHttpError extends Error {
  constructor(readonly status: number, message: string) { super(message); this.name = 'SyncHttpError' }
}

export type PullOptions = { sinceDate?: string }

export type SyncTransport = {
  deviceId: string
  push: (body: PushRequest) => Promise<PushResponse>
  pull: (table: PullTable, since: number, opts?: PullOptions) => Promise<PullResponse>
  /** `clear: true` consumes the flag; the plain read is used for the clock and must not swallow an order (M-2). */
  inboxFlag: (clear: boolean) => Promise<InboxFlagResponse>
}

async function readValue(db: RemoteDb, key: string): Promise<string | null> {
  const row = await db.select().from(s.syncState).where(eq(s.syncState.key, key)).get()
  return row?.value ?? null
}

/** null when this tablet has not been registered with a server yet (Task 12). */
export async function createTransport(db: RemoteDb, deps: ApiDeps): Promise<SyncTransport | null> {
  const [baseUrl, token, deviceId] = await Promise.all([readValue(db, SYNC_KEYS.baseUrl), readValue(db, SYNC_KEYS.token), localDeviceId(db)])
  if (baseUrl === null || token === null || deviceId === null) return null
  const headers = { 'content-type': 'application/json', authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, 'x-device-id': deviceId }

  async function call(path: string, init?: RequestInit): Promise<unknown> {
    const res = await deps.fetch(`${baseUrl}${path}`, { ...init, headers })
    if (!res.ok) throw new SyncHttpError(res.status, `${res.status} ${await res.text().catch(() => '')}`.slice(0, 300))
    return res.json()
  }

  return {
    deviceId,
    push: async (body) => PushResponse.parse(await call('/sync/push', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(body) })),
    pull: async (table, since, opts) => {
      const date = opts?.sinceDate === undefined ? '' : `&sinceDate=${opts.sinceDate}`
      return PullResponse.parse(await call(`/sync/pull?table=${table}&since=${since}${date}`))
    },
    inboxFlag: async (clear) => (await call(`/sync/inbox-flag${clear ? '?clear=1' : ''}`)) as InboxFlagResponse,
  }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `apps/pos/src/sync/push.ts`**

```ts
import { asc, eq } from 'drizzle-orm'
import type { RemoteDb } from '@dayo/db-schema/browser'
import * as s from '@dayo/db-schema/sqlite'
import { MAX_PUSH_ROWS, type PushRow, type SyncTable } from '@dayo/contracts'
import type { ApiDeps } from '../api/deps'
import { encodeLastError, MAX_DEFERRED_ATTEMPTS } from './keys'
import type { SyncTransport } from './transport'

export type PushOutcome = { sent: number; dead: number; deferred: number }

/**
 * spec §6.1: FIFO, at most 200 rows per request, one idempotency key per row (the key the writer stored — plan 3 M7).
 * `accepted`/`duplicate` → sent · `rejected` → dead letter with the reason (never retried automatically, Q5-12) ·
 * `deferred` → stays pending, and after MAX_DEFERRED_ATTEMPTS tries it joins the dead list so somebody sees it (I-1).
 */
export async function pushOnce(db: RemoteDb, deps: ApiDeps, transport: SyncTransport): Promise<PushOutcome> {
  const total: PushOutcome = { sent: 0, dead: 0, deferred: 0 }
  for (;;) {
    const batch = await db.select().from(s.outbox).where(eq(s.outbox.status, 'pending'))
      .orderBy(asc(s.outbox.createdAt), asc(s.outbox.id)).limit(MAX_PUSH_ROWS).all()
    if (batch.length === 0) return total

    const rows: PushRow[] = batch.map((r) => ({ idempotencyKey: r.idempotencyKey, table: r.tableName as SyncTable, row: r.rowJson as Record<string, unknown> }))
    const answer = await transport.push({ deviceId: transport.deviceId, rows })
    const verdictOf = new Map(answer.results.map((r) => [r.idempotencyKey, r]))
    const at = deps.now()
    const round: PushOutcome = { sent: 0, dead: 0, deferred: 0 }   // per round, not cumulative (I-2)

    for (const queued of batch) {
      const verdict = verdictOf.get(queued.idempotencyKey)
      const attempts = queued.attempts + 1
      if (verdict === undefined || verdict.status === 'deferred') {
        const reason = verdict?.reason ?? 'NO_ANSWER'
        const detail = verdict?.detail ?? 'the server did not answer for this row'
        if (attempts >= MAX_DEFERRED_ATTEMPTS) {
          await db.update(s.outbox).set({ status: 'dead', deadAt: at, attempts, lastError: encodeLastError(reason, detail) }).where(eq(s.outbox.id, queued.id))
          round.dead++
        } else {
          await db.update(s.outbox).set({ attempts, lastError: encodeLastError(reason, detail) }).where(eq(s.outbox.id, queued.id))
          round.deferred++
        }
      } else if (verdict.status === 'rejected') {
        await db.update(s.outbox).set({ status: 'dead', deadAt: at, attempts, lastError: encodeLastError(verdict.reason ?? 'REJECTED', verdict.detail ?? '') }).where(eq(s.outbox.id, queued.id))
        round.dead++
      } else {
        await db.update(s.outbox).set({ status: 'sent', sentAt: at, attempts, lastError: null }).where(eq(s.outbox.id, queued.id))
        round.sent++
      }
    }

    total.sent += round.sent
    total.dead += round.dead
    total.deferred += round.deferred
    // No progress this round means the next round would send exactly the same rows — stop (I-2).
    if (round.sent === 0 && round.dead === 0) return total
    if (batch.length < MAX_PUSH_ROWS) return total
  }
}
```

```ts
  | 'SYNC_OFFLINE'        // the server could not be reached — rows stay pending
  | 'SYNC_UNAUTHORIZED'   // the device token was revoked or is wrong — register again
  | 'NOT_REGISTERED'      // this tablet has never been connected to a server
  | 'ALREADY_REGISTERED'  // it is already connected
```

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { eq } from 'drizzle-orm'
import * as s from '@dayo/db-schema/sqlite'
import { posErrorCode } from '../src/api/errors'
import { SYNC_KEYS } from '../src/sync/keys'
import { openReadyApi, sellSku, type ReadyApi } from './helpers/db'
import { fakeServer, markRegistered, type FakeServer } from './helpers/sync'

let t: ReadyApi
let server: FakeServer

beforeEach(async () => {
  server = fakeServer()
  t = await openReadyApi({ fetch: server.fetch })
  await markRegistered(t.db)
})

async function cursorOf(table: string): Promise<string | null> {
  const row = await t.db.select().from(s.syncState).where(eq(s.syncState.key, SYNC_KEYS.cursor(table))).get()
  return row?.value ?? null
}

describe('pullOnce', () => {
  it('asks for every pull table starting at cursor 0 the first time', async () => {
    await t.api.syncNow()
    expect(server.calls.some((c) => c.includes('table=item&since=0'))).toBe(true)
  })

  it('applies a changed reference row on top of the seeded one (the server always wins — spec §6.1)', async () => {
    const before = await t.db.select().from(s.item).where(eq(s.item.code, 'RM-TEA-01')).get()
    server.pullRows.set('item', { rows: [{ ...before!, name: 'ชาไทยผง (ปรับชื่อ)', version: 2, updatedAt: '2026-09-17T04:00:00.000Z' }], nextSince: 7 })
    await t.api.syncNow()
    expect((await t.db.select().from(s.item).where(eq(s.item.code, 'RM-TEA-01')).get())?.name).toBe('ชาไทยผง (ปรับชื่อ)')
    expect(await cursorOf('item')).toBe('7')
  })

  it('remembers the cursor per table and sends it next time', async () => {
    server.pullRows.set('price', { rows: [], nextSince: 42 })
    await t.api.syncNow()
    server.calls.length = 0
    await t.api.syncNow()
    expect(server.calls.some((c) => c.includes('table=price&since=42'))).toBe(true)
  })

  it('stores a pulled bill whose shift this tablet does not have, without the shift link (re-review §7)', async () => {
    const channel = await t.db.select().from(s.channel).get()
    const old = {
      id: 'old-1', origin: 'device', deviceId: 'dev-gone', receiptNo: 'B-000001', queueNo: 1, businessDate: '2026-09-01',
      shiftId: 'sh-that-this-tablet-never-had', channelId: channel!.id, customerId: null, status: 'paid',
      subtotalSatang: 6500, discountSatang: 0, totalSatang: 6500, vatSatang: 0, costSatang: 1800, note: null,
      createdByType: 'user', createdById: t.owner.id, createdAt: '2026-09-01T03:00:00.000Z', paidAt: '2026-09-01T03:00:00.000Z', readyAt: null, voidedAt: null,
    }
    server.pullRows.set('order', { rows: [old], nextSince: 11 })
    await t.api.syncNow()
    const stored = await t.db.select().from(s.order).where(eq(s.order.id, 'old-1')).get()
    expect(stored).toBeDefined()
    expect(stored?.shiftId).toBeNull()
  })

  it('keeps the shift link when the tablet does have that shift', async () => {
    const order = await t.db.select().from(s.order).where(eq(s.order.shiftId, t.shift.id)).get()
    if (order) {
      server.pullRows.set('order', { rows: [{ ...order }], nextSince: 12 })
      await t.api.syncNow()
      expect((await t.db.select().from(s.order).where(eq(s.order.id, order.id)).get())?.shiftId).toBe(t.shift.id)
    }
  })

  it('applies a pulled price so the next sale is priced from it (plan 3 hand-off · I-13 · N-i8)', async () => {
    const price = await t.db.select().from(s.price).get()
    expect(price).toBeDefined()
    const raised = price!.amountSatang + 500
    server.pullRows.set('price', { rows: [{ ...price!, amountSatang: raised, version: 2 }], nextSince: 21 })
    await t.api.syncNow()
    const menu = await t.api.loadMenu()
    expect(menu.variants.some((v) => v.priceSatang === raised)).toBe(true)
  })

  it('answers PRICE_CHANGED when the cart was priced before that pull (plan 3 M-3)', async () => {
    const variant = await t.db.select().from(s.productVariant).where(eq(s.productVariant.sku, 'Original-16oz')).get()
    const sweet = await t.db.select().from(s.sweetnessLevel).where(eq(s.sweetnessLevel.code, 'S050')).get()
    const shownTotal = (await t.api.loadMenu()).variants.find((v) => v.id === variant!.id)!.priceSatang
    const price = await t.db.select().from(s.price).where(eq(s.price.variantId, variant!.id)).get()
    server.pullRows.set('price', { rows: [{ ...price!, amountSatang: shownTotal + 700, version: 9 }], nextSince: 31 })
    await t.api.syncNow()
    await expect(t.api.commitSale({
      orderId: t.deps.newId(), actorUserId: t.owner.id,
      lines: [{ variantId: variant!.id, sweetnessId: sweet!.id, qty: 1 }],
      discount: null, payment: { method: 'CASH', tenderedSatang: 100_000 }, expectedTotalSatang: shownTotal,
    })).rejects.toSatisfy((e: unknown) => posErrorCode(e) === 'PRICE_CHANGED')
  })

  it('applies a pulled setting so the tablet stops using its built-in default (plan 3b hand-off)', async () => {
    server.pullRows.set('setting', { rows: [{ key: 'cash.variance_alert_satang', valueJson: 5_000, effectiveFrom: '2026-09-17T00:00:00.000Z', updatedAt: '2026-09-17T00:00:00.000Z', version: 1 }], nextSince: 3 })
    await t.api.syncNow()
    const row = await t.db.select().from(s.setting).where(eq(s.setting.key, 'cash.variance_alert_satang')).get()
    expect(row?.valueJson).toBe(5_000)
  })

  it('inserts a server-written order without touching one the device already has', async () => {
    const channel = await t.db.select().from(s.channel).get()
    const serverOrder = {
      id: 'srv-1', origin: 'server', deviceId: null, receiptNo: null, queueNo: null, businessDate: '2026-09-17',
      shiftId: null, channelId: channel!.id, customerId: null, status: 'pending_payment', subtotalSatang: 6500,
      discountSatang: 0, totalSatang: 6500, vatSatang: 0, costSatang: 0, note: null, createdByType: 'system',
      createdById: 'server', createdAt: '2026-09-17T03:30:00.000Z', paidAt: null, readyAt: null, voidedAt: null,
    }
    server.pullRows.set('order', { rows: [serverOrder], nextSince: 3 })
    await t.api.syncNow()
    expect((await t.db.select().from(s.order).where(eq(s.order.id, 'srv-1')).get())?.origin).toBe('server')
  })

  it('never overwrites a local bill with the copy that comes back down', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, { method: 'CASH', tenderedSatang: 10_000 })
    const order = await t.db.select().from(s.order).get()
    server.pullRows.set('order', { rows: [{ ...order!, status: 'cancelled' }], nextSince: 5 })
    await t.api.syncNow()
    expect((await t.db.select().from(s.order).where(eq(s.order.id, order!.id)).get())?.status).toBe(order!.status)
  })

  it('records the clock skew from the server time (spec §6.3)', async () => {
    t.clock.set('2026-09-17T03:20:00.000Z')
    server.serverTime = '2026-09-17T03:00:00.000Z'
    const run = await t.api.syncNow()
    expect(run.clockSkewMs).toBe(20 * 60 * 1000)
    expect((await t.api.syncStatus()).clockOff).toBe(true)
  })

  it('reads the clock without consuming the inbox flag (M-2)', async () => {
    server.hasInbox = true
    await t.api.syncNow()
    expect(server.calls.filter((c) => c.includes('inbox-flag?clear=1'))).toHaveLength(0)
    expect(server.hasInbox).toBe(true)
  })

  it('does not call pull when the push failed offline', async () => {
    server.offline = true
    await t.api.syncNow()
    expect(server.calls.filter((c) => c.includes('/sync/pull'))).toHaveLength(0)
  })
})
```

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { openReadyApi, sellSku, type ReadyApi } from './helpers/db'
import { fakeServer, markRegistered, outboxRows, type FakeServer } from './helpers/sync'

let t: ReadyApi
let server: FakeServer
const CASH = { method: 'CASH' as const, tenderedSatang: 10_000 }

beforeEach(async () => {
  server = fakeServer()
  t = await openReadyApi({ fetch: server.fetch })
  await markRegistered(t.db)
})

describe('syncNow turns transport failures into codes the screen can show (moved here from Task 9 — N-i4)', () => {
  it('reports SYNC_OFFLINE and leaves everything pending when the network is down', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    server.offline = true
    const run = await t.api.syncNow()
    expect(run.ok).toBe(false)
    expect(run.error).toBe('SYNC_OFFLINE')
    expect((await outboxRows(t.db, 'pending')).length).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(await outboxRows(t.db, 'dead')).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('reports SYNC_UNAUTHORIZED when the token was revoked, without killing rows', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    server.unauthorized = true
    const run = await t.api.syncNow()
    expect(run.error).toBe('SYNC_UNAUTHORIZED')
    expect(await outboxRows(t.db, 'dead')).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('reports NOT_REGISTERED and calls nothing when the tablet has no server yet', async () => {
    const fresh = await openReadyApi({ fetch: server.fetch })       // no markRegistered
    await sellSku(fresh, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    expect((await fresh.api.syncNow()).error).toBe('NOT_REGISTERED')
    expect(server.pushes).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('reports what it pushed and what died', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    server.verdict = (r) => (r.table === 'payment' ? { status: 'rejected', reason: 'SCHEMA', detail: 'x' } : { status: 'accepted' })
    const run = await t.api.syncNow()
    expect(run.ok).toBe(true)
    expect(run.dead).toBe(1)
    expect(run.pushed).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(5)
  })
})

describe('syncTick (spec §6.2)', () => {
  it('"push" only pushes', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    await t.api.syncTick('push')
    expect(server.calls.some((c) => c.includes('/sync/push'))).toBe(true)
    expect(server.calls.some((c) => c.includes('/sync/pull'))).toBe(false)
  })

  it('"flag" consumes the flag and pulls only the bill tables when it is set', async () => {
    await t.api.syncTick('flag')
    expect(server.calls).toEqual(['GET /sync/inbox-flag?clear=1'])
    server.hasInbox = true
    server.calls.length = 0
    await t.api.syncTick('flag')
    expect(server.calls.filter((c) => c.includes('/sync/pull')).length).toBe(4)
  })

  it('"pull" pulls every table', async () => {
    await t.api.syncTick('pull')
    expect(server.calls.filter((c) => c.includes('/sync/pull')).length).toBeGreaterThan(10)
  })
})

describe('syncStatus and dead letters', () => {
  it('counts what is waiting as documents, the way the sell screen badge does', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    const before = await t.api.syncStatus()
    expect(before.pendingItems).toBe(2)          // the open shift (openReadyApi) + this bill — I-14
    expect(before.lastSyncAt).toBeNull()
    await t.api.syncNow()
    const after = await t.api.syncStatus()
    expect(after.pendingItems).toBe(0)
    expect(after.lastSyncAt).not.toBeNull()
  })

  it('lists a dead row with its reason code and the document it belongs to', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    server.verdict = (r) => (r.table === 'payment' ? { status: 'rejected', reason: 'TOTALS', detail: 'sum(payment) 1 != total 6500' } : { status: 'accepted' })
    await t.api.syncNow()
    const dead = await t.api.listDeadRows()
    expect(dead).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(dead[0]).toMatchObject({ table: 'payment', reason: 'TOTALS' })
    expect(dead[0]?.detail).toContain('6500')
    expect((await t.api.syncStatus()).deadItems).toBe(1)
  })

  it('re-queues a dead row when the owner presses "ส่งใหม่" (Q5-12)', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    server.verdict = (r) => (r.table === 'payment' ? { status: 'rejected', reason: 'SCHEMA', detail: 'x' } : { status: 'accepted' })
    await t.api.syncNow()
    const [dead] = await t.api.listDeadRows()
    server.verdict = () => ({ status: 'accepted' })
    await t.api.retryDeadRow(dead!.id)
    expect(await outboxRows(t.db, 'pending')).toHaveLength(1)
    await t.api.syncNow()
    expect(await t.api.listDeadRows()).toHaveLength(0)
  })

  it('resets the attempt count when a dead row is re-queued, so it gets a fair run', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    server.verdict = (r) => (r.table === 'payment' ? { status: 'rejected', reason: 'SCHEMA', detail: 'x' } : { status: 'accepted' })
    await t.api.syncNow()
    const [dead] = await t.api.listDeadRows()
    await t.api.retryDeadRow(dead!.id)
    expect((await outboxRows(t.db, 'pending'))[0]?.attempts).toBe(0)
  })

  it('never deletes a dead row and never re-sends it by itself', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    server.verdict = (r) => (r.table === 'payment' ? { status: 'rejected', reason: 'SCHEMA', detail: 'x' } : { status: 'accepted' })
    await t.api.syncNow()
    await t.api.syncNow()
    expect(await outboxRows(t.db, 'dead')).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(server.pushes.flatMap((p) => p.rows).filter((r) => r.table === 'payment')).toHaveLength(1)
  })

  it('tells bootstrap what the sell screen needs', async () => {
    await sellSku(t, 'Original-16oz', 1, CASH)
    server.verdict = (r) => (r.table === 'payment' ? { status: 'rejected', reason: 'SCHEMA', detail: 'x' } : { status: 'accepted' })
    await t.api.syncNow()
    const boot = await t.api.bootstrap()
    expect(boot.deadSyncItems).toBe(1)
    expect(boot.registered).toBe(true)
    expect(boot.lastSyncAt).not.toBeNull()
  })
})
```
